```
### 新增  - 右侧栏新增「文件」：在终端旁浏览、上传、下载和编辑远程文件，直接使用终端的连接，不用再次登录。 - 「文件」跟随终端的当前目录，bash、zsh 可一键自动配置。 - SFTP 远程侧新增「查找文件」。 - SFTP 文件列表可选彩色图标（设置 › SFTP › 图标风格）。 - 标签显示连接状态：连接中黄点、断开红点；远程终端断开后按 Enter 即可重连。  ### 改进  - 服务器主机密钥变更时，可在核验指纹后选择「更新并连接」，无需手动修改 known_hosts。 - 主机名称可以留空，默认使用连接地址。  ### 修复  - 修复 Windows 下部分堡垒机配置 ShellRS 为 Xshell 或 WinSCP 客户端时，提示「请使用官方客户端」的问题。 - 修复连接 Windows 主机时标签不显示 Windows 图标的问题。

## [6.4.0](https://github.com/morluto/rea/compare/rea-agents-6.3.0...rea-agents-6.4.0) (2026-10-11)  ### Highlights  - Android: added device inspection, split-APK acquisition, logs, screenshots, file transfers and explicit app lifecycle actions through ADB, plus manifest and localized-string decoding with Apktool. ([#1552](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/1552), [#1556](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/1556)) Thanks @3QL. - JEB: browse project units and decompile selected items through a caller-started local JEB MCP server. ([#1536](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/1536)) Thanks @3QL. - Large analyses: discover retained Evidence without loading every payload, and inspect JavaScript graphs, binary layouts and function dossiers through summaries and selected views. ([#1682](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/1682), [#1568](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/1568), [#1570](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/1570), [#1345](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/1345), [#1507](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/1507), [#1247](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/1247)) Thanks @jhuang-tw, @kaoru0822-kitauji, @N0zoM1z0. - JavaScript: isolate source analysis in an owned process with configurable heap and timeout limits; retain completed facts and explicit unknowns when analysis runs out of resources. ([#1651](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/1651), [#1563](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/1563), [#1565](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/1565)) Thanks @N0zoM1z0, @peter-research. - Ghidra: configure import language/compiler specifications and seed functions, code and labels before auto-analysis. ([#1667](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/1667)) Thanks @sqmagellan. - RPC discovery: recognize supported tRPC calls, HTTP-link URLs and named GraphQL operations in JavaScript applications and web bundles. ([#1670](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/1670)) Thanks @pompiliodonatoapp.  ### Changes  - Added Ghidra support for Windows PE DLLs and bounded MIPS ELF analysis, including carved MIPS inputs with incomplete section tables. Windows P0 remains read-only. ([#1303](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/1303), [#1362](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/1362), [#1629](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/1629), [#1290](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/1290), [#718](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/718), [#1619](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/1619)) Thanks @bqw371, @jaelliot, @kvalls, @N0zoM1z0. - Enabled `set_address_name` and `set_addresses_names` for Ghidra sessions with mutation authority, including namespace-aware data labels and per-address batch results. ([#1483](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/1483), [#1583](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/1583), [#1252](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/1252), [#1253](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/1253), [modelcontextprotocol/typescript-sdk#2997](https://github.com/modelcontextprotocol/typescript-sdk/issues/2997)) Thanks @N0zoM1z0, @nikolay-dementiev, @sathariels. - Improved Codex onboarding and recovery from observed client, PATH and provider blockers. ([80896a1](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/80896a1d64b216ef893907249cd4252f86215d49), [c916850](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/c9168508527302ac57bc86385ccf0d824697b5dc)) Thanks @Y0UW1SH. - CLI/MCP errors: accept JSON files for web comparisons, preserve native option help and union correction details, remove invalid aliases and validate doctor against its discovered output contract. ([#1397](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/1397), [#1395](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/1395), [#1425](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/1425), [#1461](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/1461), [8b4c96e](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/8b4c96e6c6b3ed6806e4cd72b556b5db6114304a), [#1421](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/1421), [#1454](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/1454)) Thanks @Fazmin, @kaoru0822-kitauji, @N0zoM1z0.  ### ⚠ BREAKING CHANGES  - WebSocket results: use `network.websocket_connections` instead of `network.websocket_events`. Connections contain `request_id`, `url` and `events`; allow `payload_bytes: null` when size is unknown. No legacy alias remains. ([db4e360](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/db4e3608465486466591b594cf77037ac150c988)) - Direct resource APIs: pass a caller-owned `ArtifactResourceScope` to inventory/extraction, JavaScript analysis, reference imports and web exports; retain it through cleanup/shutdown. For `readJavaScriptArtifactFiles`, scope is argument three and abort signal is argument four. Keep each root inventory source’s single cleanup owner. Use `OwnedFileHandle` owners, injected ASAR `fd`/`read` handles instead of `createReadStream`, VCS `Result` values, and `WebScriptExportError`’s options object. CLI/MCP inputs for the scoped operations are unchanged. ([ffe0574](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/ffe0574e17b9d973860371083cb1a351be912131), [53e7fad](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/53e7fadbd078422ba3d55b5499a70d237c5f4908), [684199b](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/684199ba0be7822266d57ddb8a40e95f087fdc42), [be75ed5](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/be75ed5e4b4bfb146af497f8fa5d172a1ea19462), [be6272f](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/be6272f26115bd8e94f2ab0df4cf092004587a18), [e0e49e9](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/e0e49e9dd53d9d1f1d2330b23f7f880dc1c81774), [95a77b6](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/95a77b6e25420b3879bd0e32bdbb14d452b38776)) - Saved reference graphs: exclusions require their matching `pattern` and distinguish `project-ignored` from `default-ignored`. Re-import graphs missing that provenance. ([6585293](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/6585293c562853b95c3a70df02c12ad8cc17afc4)) - Terminal captures: keep initial and resized buffers within 1,000,000 cells using `columns × (rows + scrollback)`; oversized scenarios are rejected before launch. ([f9ea9a9](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/f9ea9a9268c854d31048da9f8a50cfa37703fe5a)) - Optional agent-verification reports: accept schema version 4, nullable `expectedFirstTool` and `targetClarificationPassed`. Production CLI/MCP contracts are unchanged. ([#1613](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/1613)) - Capture comparisons: recapture both sides with matching `literal-replacements-v1` semantics when custom normalization patterns are used. Legacy unversioned pattern captures remain readable but cannot be compared with new pattern captures; captures without patterns are unaffected. ([#1456](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/1456), [2b6f14f](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/2b6f14f528297cf12c776c960af0859f5cfbefbf)) Thanks @Madhavan006, @N0zoM1z0. - Artifact graph edges: read child artifact IDs and paths from the occurrence selected by `occurrence_id`, not edge fields `child_artifact_id`/`logical_path`. Earlier inventory Evidence no longer validates against the new commitment. ([#1493](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/1493)) Thanks @kaoru0822-kitauji. - Semantic graph schema: nodes and relations no longer include `identifier_strategy`, and relation IDs no longer hash it. Earlier Evidence no longer validates against this schema. ([#1474](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/1474)) Thanks @kaoru0822-kitauji. - Semantic identities: select IDs from the parent graph; read exact event names from `event_name`, endpoints from `endpoint` and literal values from `properties.value`. New analyses use digest-based IDs for long/reserved event and endpoint names and `value-sha256` literal role keys, with shorter labels; string literal labels now read `string literal`. Previously stored graphs retain their original IDs and remain valid workflow inputs. ([#1427](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/1427), [#1423](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/1423), [#1407](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/1407)) Thanks @N0zoM1z0.  ### Contributors  - Thanks also to @BaaRai, @commit-regrets, @coygeek, @Franky100-pig, @inii-sayaa, @jopemachine, @JosephDemarest, @keemsisi, @Totopo27 for contributions included in this release.  Full contribution record: [336 commits and 163 merged PRs](https://github.com/morluto/rea/blob/rea-agents-6.4.0/docs/releases/6.4.0.contributions.json).

## 1.3.167 — 2026-10-14  - 官方档在调用方没有 system 时也出站四段：计费头、身份句、官方 prompt、Environment。顺序跟 cli-node 一致，计费头在 prompt 前面。调用方自己的 system 仍放在第 5 段。 - `rewrite` 仍把计费头放到 cache 断点之后。线上档是 official，不走那条。  已部署机升级：更新 Node 控制面，重启一次 Node。无迁移、无新依赖，不必 `wrap-cli/sync`。不要覆盖 `routing.json`、`vms/`、`data/`、`.env`。  无 system 的 `claude-sonnet-5-5` 调试日志确认出站 4 段、`tools_count=0`：计费头、身份句、官方 prompt（1h global）、`# Environment`。随后把用户正文加长，`cache_read` 到 105874。这是用户正文命中，不是 Claude Code 工具尾。  [CHANGELOG.md](https://github.com/dofastted/vm2api/blob/v1.3.167/CHANGELOG.md)

### 2026-10-12T02:42:34Z [**AnnaSuSu/OpenContextEngine**](https://github.com/AnnaSuSu/OpenContextEngine)

```
Overlapping workspace roots previously built separate copies of the same code data, and a first-time build could delay updates to an existing index. Version 0.1.10 shares immutable source text and embedding vectors, gives existing-workspace updates priority, and makes file coverage inspectable.  - Reuse indexed data across parent and child workspaces in either order. Each workspace retains its own file manifest, structural graph, and relative search paths; searches score only that workspace's units. - Coordinate shared-cache writers across processes to avoid duplicate embedding requests. Workspace manifests and temporary build pins protect data during reclamation, including while a first-time build yields to updates. - Prioritize updates to published indexes at both the global build limit and shared-cache writer lock. First-time builds finish their in-flight embedding requests before yielding, then resume from cached vectors. - Add optional `path`, `limit`, and `cursor` arguments to `index_status`. Inspect a file or directory for indexed, pending, excluded, deleted, or missing entries, with exclusion reasons and bounded pagination. Coverage checks perform no model requests and do not wake idle indexing. - Persist `OCE_SHARED_INDEX_DIRECTORY` for custom cache locations and tolerate Windows file-probe cleanup contention without leaking build capacity.  Upgrade:  sh npm install -g open-context-engine@0.1.10   Restart connected MCP clients after upgrading. Existing indexes remain on disk. The first synchronization builds canonical-path vectors once; subsequent overlapping workspaces reuse them. Shared pools are isolated by embedding provider, model, dimensions, and revision. Default data is stored in `~/.cache/opencontextengine/shared-index`; custom state locations use `.shared-index` under their state parent unless `OCE_SHARED_INDEX_DIRECTORY` is configured.  Example `index_status` arguments:  json {"directory_path":"/absolute/path/to/repository","path":"src/session.py"}   Use `"path":"."` to inspect the workspace and `nextCursor` for another page. [Usage and configuration](https://github.com/AnnaSuSu/OpenContextEngine/blob/v0.1.10/docs/QUICKSTART.md#index-coverage).  Validation: a clean checkout passed 76 Node tests and 110 Python tests on macOS (three platform-specific skips). [Linux, macOS, and Windows CI](https://github.com/AnnaSuSu/OpenContextEngine/actions/runs/38196088666) passed for the release commit. The exact archive passed isolated installation, both CLI aliases, fresh Python setup and doctor, and real-model MCP checks for parent/child reuse, saved edits, coverage, pagination, environment-file exclusion, and restart restoration.  The verified npm archive and its SHA-256 checksum are attached. 
```

### Changes  - library: Grok Build and Grok App can be given hooks (#1790) - agent, gui: connecting an agent whose model list holds none of magpie's models says so, in the reader's language (#1989, znuaibot) - cli: a magpie model picked for an agent not connected connects it first (#1900) - tests: the cache-fields record test starts from a log of its own (#1897) - tests: the slow-read list's immediate re-ask is asked within its own retry window (#1889) - gateway: Codex's 507 "exceeded request buffer limit" is asked again and told as a 503, not relayed as it came (#2034, sparrowu93) - gateway: an image request to a routing group moves on to the next member when one answers 5xx (#2005, tianxia3111) - usage: each account picks a recorded allowance cycle beside its curve, meters and legend following it (#2009, NorethSea) - agent: Claude Code's /model takes models added in magpie again when the picker magpie wrote has lost its mark (#2046, cjyrainbow) - gui: magpie web keeps its sign-in when another service on the same address fills or clears the host's cookies, and a page opened without it says why and takes the key (#2033, 321sssrt-bit) - agent: T3 Code keeps the name, auto-compact and other settings the user gave magpie's provider across a restart (#2030, Kanscape) - gateway: a video request's other fields reach a provider's own videos API as sent, so Agnes gets its mode (#1399, shen-joe) - dsh: Reconnect puts magpie's key over one in dsh's key store that magpie refuses, and Disconnect puts it back (#1419, DannyJRu) - gateway: a translated Responses reply keeps the web search that ran, with its query and sources (#2044, BussanQ) - agent: OmO's update runs the npm of the node it was installed with, not a system one first on magpie's PATH (#2021, ggbdpq) - library: Claude Code's hooks can go on every event it has, StopFailure, PostCompact and FileChanged among them (#2020, emo172) - gateway: a provider's own cap on how long a key that keeps failing rests (#2008, Gaudi-XIX) - gateway: a key out of a daily allowance rests until the time of day it comes back (#2008, Gaudi-XIX) - gateway: a plugin whose config hook says searches: true is sent the client's web_search as sent (#2027, yudaleng) - factory: Haiku 5.5 and Mistral Large 4, in droid 0.240.0's picker, are offered, and moved users get plugin 0.1.25 (JL on Discord) - davsync: a computer joining a sync folder keeps the gateway keys the folder's store has never seen (#1737, #1714, ggbdpq) - sessions: a gateway-sessions-hidden.json that doesn't parse is kept aside before the next delete or Restore writes over it (#1722, ggbdpq) - mcpauth: an MCP sign-in canceled while its token is being fetched no longer saves the late token or replaces the server's sign-in (#1750, ggbdpq) - provider: a Codex reset spent while an allowance read is in flight is never undone by that read, even on Windows' coarse clock (#1754, ggbdpq)  ### Install  Download from [usemagpie.ai](https://usemagpie.ai), or `curl -fsSL https://usemagpie.ai/install.sh | sh`. An installed magpie updates itself.  - **macOS (Apple Silicon):** `magpie-darwin-arm64.dmg` - **macOS (Intel):** `magpie-darwin-amd64.dmg` - **Windows:** `magpie-windows-amd64.exe` (ARM: `magpie-windows-arm64.exe`) - **Linux:** `magpie-linux-amd64` / `magpie-linux-arm64` (needs GTK 3 and WebKitGTK 4.1) - **Terminal only:** `magpie-cli-<os>-<arch>`    --- The same release, and the feed magpie updates from: https://github.com/yetone/magpie-releases/releases/tag/v0.1.1230

### 2026-10-07T17:04:01Z [**nyakang/nyaterm**](https://github.com/nyakang/nyaterm)

```
## NyaTerm v1.3.0  `v1.3.0` 是 NyaTerm 稳定版的一次较大更新。除了新增 Web Beta 和插件 Marketplace 基础能力，本版还集中改进了 AI 上下文、串口、RDP/VNC、快捷命令与安全凭据管理，并修复多项长时间会话、同步和 Windows 使用问题。  ### ✨ 新增功能  - **Web Beta（PR #843）**：新增可通过 Docker / Compose 部署的浏览器运行时，支持 SSH、Telnet、VNC、代理与 SSH 跳板，以及 SFTP 浏览、单文件上传/下载、内部文本编辑和备份导入导出等常用远程管理能力。 - **插件 Marketplace**：新增 Marketplace 界面与 Store 包安装链路，安装前可审核包信息，并对 Store 包执行固定公钥、目录元数据和包内容校验；本地 `.nyap` 仍可作为本地插件安装。同步加入独立插件创作 CLI 与模板基础。 - **AI 上下文与代理**：AI 请求可结合当前连接、分组和运行时会话信息构造更准确的目标上下文，并继续对敏感信息脱敏（#481）；新增 System / Direct / Custom 三种 AI 代理模式，自定义代理支持 HTTP、SOCKS5、认证和 bypass。 - **串口流控**：串口连接支持 None、XON/XOFF 软件流控和 RTS/CTS 硬件流控，并可随连接保存（#794）。 - **Windows RDP 客户端选择**：Windows 可选择使用 NyaTerm 内置 RDP 或系统 `mstsc.exe` 打开保存的 RDP 连接；外部启动只传主机和端口，不传保存的凭据（#815，PR #847）。 - **快捷命令操作**：完善空白区域和条目的右键菜单，支持新建命令、新建根分类、复制命令，并可继承当前保存分类（#845，PR #846）。 - **SSH 密钥 / 密码排序**：安全凭据列表支持拖拽自定义排序并持久化（#738）。 - **外部包管理器支持（PR #826）**：由外部包管理器构建/分发的版本可声明更新来源，应用会避免与外部更新机制冲突。  ### 🐛 问题修复  - 修复长时间运行全屏 TUI 后转录重放状态无界增长、CPU 占用升高并最终导致 SSH 输入和关闭逐渐失去响应的问题（#841，PR #842）。 - 修复 VNC 无法连接部分树莓派设备的问题，新增 RA2_256 认证兼容和服务器 RSA 公钥确认/记忆流程（#625，PR #830）。 - 修复导出的 `PROMPT_COMMAND` 使 `su`、子 Bash 或 zellij pane 中持续出现 `__nyaterm_prompt: command not found` 的问题（#694，PR #827）。 - 修复 Gitee Gist 容量达到上限时清理/恢复可能破坏远端 head 或留下无效 snapshot 引用的问题，并完善覆盖写入时的容量计算（PR #823，关联 #441）。 - 修复 Windows 串口每次写入后 flush 导致连续输入回显和回车响应卡顿的问题（#795）。 - 修复多标签/分屏持久化索引与恢复顺序不一致，避免恢复后文件发送等操作可能指向错误服务器（#819）。 - 修复快捷命令编辑器中 `--` 等内容受字体连字影响显示异常的问题，并保留选择区与撤销/重做状态（#824）。 - 修复 NyaAgent 每轮对话重新初始化、同一 AI 会话无法延续历史消息的问题（#825）。 - 改进 Windows 本地终端环境变量读取与 `%VAR%` 展开，降低修改系统/用户环境变量后必须重启 NyaTerm 才能生效的问题（#821）。 - 修复插件 Store 目录接口调整后的加载地址，以及快捷命令 SVG 元素上的上下文菜单目标识别。  ### ⚡ 性能与稳定性优化  - 远程资源监控按会话缓存最近快照，切换多个服务器时可立即恢复对应数据并只轮询当前会话，减少反复重新加载（#828）。 - 优化终端 Gutter 布局计算与刷新，减少 Codex CLI 等动态输出场景中的光标跳动和输入闪烁（#742）。 - 强化会话消息循环、输出管理和原子状态更新，降低高输出与并发状态切换下的竞争和卡顿。 - Web SSH 持续输出路径补充背压与停止顺序回归覆盖，提升高输出场景稳定性。  ### ♻️ 重构与优化  - 抽取共享 `nyaterm-core`，让桌面运行时与 Web Beta 复用连接、存储、AI、备份、SSH/VNC/Telnet 等核心能力，同时保持桌面运行路径独立。 - 完善应用窗口事件、资源监控缓存和会话状态管理的测试覆盖，降低多窗口/多会话切换时的状态串扰风险。  ### 📚 文档与国际化  - 新增 Web Beta 的 Docker / Compose 部署说明与端到端验收配置。 - 补充插件创作、打包、Marketplace 安装与签名模型说明。 - 为 AI 代理、串口流控、凭据排序等新增界面补充英文、韩文、简体中文和繁体中文文案。  ### 👥 贡献者  感谢以下外部贡献者参与本版本：  - @jeeinn — PR #823 - @sgnay — PR #826 - @developerjillur — PR #827 - @victorwon2001 — PR #830、PR #846、PR #847 - @mengshouer — PR #842  ### ⚠️ 升级说明  - **Web Beta 仍是浏览器技术预览能力**，面向单个可信管理员部署。Web 模式暂不支持本地 Shell、Serial、RDP、录制、云同步、AI Agent / MCP 和原生插件执行；这些限制不影响桌面版对应功能。 - 串口使用 **XON/XOFF 软件流控** 时，系统会禁用 XMODEM / YMODEM / ZMODEM 文件传输，因为软件流控会消费二进制传输中的控制字节。  ### 📦 版本信息  - 版本：`v1.3.0` - 通道：Stable - 分支：`main` - 上一稳定版：`v1.2.12` 
```

### 2026-10-11T16:12:31Z [**ejfkdev/ddc**](https://github.com/ejfkdev/ddc)

```
v0.1.29  **New Features** - Three new cure (0.1.4-0.1.8) source-level simplification rules, measured on three fresh random APK corpora (telegram/chatglm/cmb, 68k files):   - `dead_pure_call`: discard-result `Long.compare(a,b)` / `Objects.toString(x)` statements — the dex cmp-instruction rendering family (~63k sites)   - `trailing_else_flatten`: the else keyword after a terminal then-branch (~41k sites)   - `(T)(Object)x` → `(T)x` cast folding on Object-typed operands (the dex check-cast widening noise) - `--fast` now works on **every** Java-source command: `getclass`, `getmethod`, `pkg` (in addition to the main decompile path) — skip the simplification pass, keep formatting (~half the time)  **Bug Fixes** - Float constants emitted as raw IEEE bit patterns: dex const literals are untyped bits; in float/double arithmetic they must be reinterpreted with `from_bits`, not numerically converted (1073741824 is 2.0f, not 1.07e9f) — >27k lines of silently wrong values on telegram alone, invisible to the compile gate - JLS 6.4.2 obscured-member static-import escape: inherited field captures package name in qualified static member references (reqable variable-cannot-finds 103→5) - Seven type/scoping guards in cure found on ddc corpora (null literal overload ambiguity, for-each on non-Iterable, owner-confused method inline, enum constant shadowing, cast-position type loss, dangling cut locals, and the two first-version rule bugs in this release)  **Performance** - Four IR shape passes retired into cure (trailing-return, ternary fold, final dead assigns, bool xor): −360 lines of ddc source with gate parity - `--fast` mode: parse+print only (~10× cheaper than the simplify engine) — weibo 23.4s → 11.5s  **Quality Metrics** - Four-corpus javac gate: 8,633 errors (v0.1.28: 8,647, −14) — reqable 317 flat, weibo −1, lark −15 - Output lines: weibo 15.47M → 14.78M (−4.5% from the three new rules) - docs/quality.md (bilingual) with the E1/S1/S2/D1/P1 metric definitions and reproduction script  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/ejfkdev/ddc/compare/v0.1.28...v0.1.29 
```

# Vetta 0.5.63  发布日期：2026-10-13  ## 新增  - 添加服务商与编辑服务商改为弹窗形态，添加/编辑模型也走弹窗：服务商行右侧的加号按钮直接打开「添加模型」弹窗，弹窗内可以「测试连通」验证服务商是否可达，并支持一键从 models.dev 目录同步模型参数（显示名称、上下文窗口、最大输出、图片输入与思考档位）；目录只补空字段，手动填过的值不会被覆盖，未收录的模型会提示手动填写。模型面板底部新增「清空模型」，二次确认后一次性移除该服务商已添加的全部模型。  - 自定义服务商区块重新设计：服务商改为独立卡片（图标、接口类型、模型数与主机地址一目了然），展开后的模型列表收进内嵌面板——徽标与参数行更紧凑，编辑 / 删除操作悬停显示，默认模型星标常驻；「添加模型」与「从接口拉取」收纳为面板底部的轻量操作。模型较多时面板默认只展示 10 个，底部「查看更多（还有 N 个）」每次再展开 10 个，收起后重新从头计数。  - 设置里自定义服务商的模型列表可以直接浏览和挑选：点「从接口拉取」不再在服务商行里展开一块列表，而是从右侧滑出一个面板，一次列出接口返回的全部模型，并带上接口给出的参数（上下文窗口、最大输出、是否支持图片输入、思考及其档位）。面板内可以搜索模型名，全选 / 全不选只作用于当前筛选出来的模型；每个模型都能单独点「测试连通」确认这个服务商此刻是否可达。勾选添加时这些参数会一并写入模型配置，包括上游声明的思考档位；接口没给的字段不会被猜一个值。已经添加过的模型会被标注，也不会被重复写入。  ## 改进  - 自定义服务商从接口拉取的模型，会按需回填 models.dev 目录里的参数：网关（尤其是聚合网关）只回模型 id 时，上下文窗口、最大输出、是否支持图片输入与思考档位仍能填上。网关自己报了的字段一律以网关为准；目录里也查不到的保持留空，由 API 类型内置预设接管，不做猜测。聚合网关在模型名前叠的命名空间（`clinepass/cline-pass/deepseek-v4.1-flash` 与 `cline-pass/deepseek-v4.1-flash` 指同一个模型）会被逐层剥掉后再匹配，将来多叠一层也能命中。  - 模型的推理档位可以拖拽排序：在「编辑模型 → 推理档位」里拖动档位左侧的手柄就能调整先后，顺序即客户端展示与「默认档位」候选的顺序；默认档位始终跟着它自己所属的那一档，不会因为排序被换到别的档位上。 - 模型设置中的 OpenAI Responses 传输方式已移到页面最下方，并使用更明确的标题，避免误以为该选项会影响所有模型服务商。

# Pebrel 2.2.0  ## English  ### Added  - Added: Pane-scoped SSH local port forwarding with a Ports control and editable local/remote port dialog. Listeners bind to local loopback, bound concurrent connections, retry accept errors and stop their work when the owning session exits. ([#280](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/280)) - Added: A static administrator shield for confirmed elevated local Windows sessions, before the program or Agent icon and tab name, with matching icon size and hover text. SSH and WSL sessions retain their own identity. Addresses [#436](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/issues/436). ([#449](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/449)) - Added: Custom-theme background controls for image selection, opacity, fit, alignment and window blur, with a live preview owned by the current editor draft. ([#437](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/437)) - Added: Portable theme ZIP archives containing native theme JSON, static background images, an optional preview, author, license and version metadata. Import adds a theme to the local library; applying it remains a separate action. ([#438](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/438)) - Added: `pebrel theme pack`, `check`, `import` and `limits`, with JSON results, meaningful exit codes and Bash/Zsh/Fish command completion. Relative image paths resolve from the theme JSON directory. ([#440](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/440)) - Added: Theme-editor ZIP import/export dialogs with archive inspection, package metadata, explicit confirmation and an entry to edit the installed theme. ([#441](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/441)) - Added: Windows video and streaming GIF backgrounds with an explicit image/video/GIF source selector. Playback uses bounded decoding, frame and GPU resources, and follows window visibility and activity. ([#501](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/501)) - Added: Grain, Neon Vortex, Aurora Ribbons and Liquid Silk background presets, plus local WGSL background files. Effects require explicit activation; retaining a file path alone does not start execution. ([#501](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/501)) - Added: Ordered WGSL terminal postprocessing on supported native Windows, Linux Vulkan and macOS Metal windows. The chain supports up to eight files/eight fragment passes, explicit reload, independent activation and off/focused/always animation policies. ([#513](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/513)) - Added: Terminal-effect inputs for the visible terminal texture, viewport, monotonic time, focus, current/previous cursor and resolved 256-color palette, with cursor-trail and scanline/vignette examples. Text remains at native pixel resolution; completion and IME overlays render afterwards. ([#513](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/513)) - Added: A short circular reveal for day/night theme changes on Windows and center-expanding settings-search focus feedback. Reduced-motion preferences keep immediate changes; unavailable capture leaves theme switching functional. ([#506](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/506)) - Added: Completion from the native editor buffer and actual caret through PSReadLine, Bash Readline and Zsh ZLE, including advertised WSL/SSH startup support. Queries preserve user key bindings and distinguish typed text from shell prediction text. ([#469](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/469), [#473](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/473)) - Added: Caret-aware Git argument completion for explicit tracking options, configured remotes, branches, references and push/pull/fetch argument roles, using metadata from the selected local, WSL or authenticated SSH environment. ([#465](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/465), [#466](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/466), [#467](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/467)) - Added: npm, pnpm and Yarn workspace/script completion from the selected packages, with workspace names/paths, supported filters, dependency selection and package-manager-specific script sets. Local and remote projects share the same selection rules. ([#468](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/468)) - Added: A matching offline relay installer archive and installation guide alongside the Android APK, with Linux x64/ARM64 binaries, download entry points, product and dependency licenses, source identity and checksum verification. ([#489](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/489)) - Added: Bounded `pane.scroll` input and paged `screen_history` reads in the runtime/mobile bridge, with screen-cell validation, history identity and matching schema updates for the phone terminal viewport. ([#498](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/498), [#519](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/519))  ### Improved  - Improved: Theme previews reuse prepared images, discard outdated loads and release owned resources when the editor closes. File-picker and package results remain bound to the originating draft; import/export work stays off the UI thread. ([#437](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/437), [#441](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/441)) - Improved: Theme-package validation checks real byte limits, declared entries, resource hashes and portable paths before installation. Static image import/export shares one implementation; animated media, video and shaders are not activated or installed by this package format. ([#438](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/438), [#440](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/440), [#441](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/441)) - Improved: Background decoding, material application and terminal-effect controllers have explicit owners and shared resource budgets. The intermediate duplicate native-visual abstraction was reverted; the retained native adapters follow the existing UI lifecycle. ([#501](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/501), [#513](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/513)) - Improved: Effect availability follows the current window backend. Unsupported windows retain editable saved sources and allow disabling; animation scheduling follows focus/visibility and explicit playback policy. ([#513](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/513)) - Improved: Terminal-effect file ordering, reload and playback settings are grouped under Advanced effects. Custom-background controls appear when needed, preserving saved activation and playback choices. ([#505](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/505)) - Improved: Mobile network address, port and refresh action share one row. Dropdown hover remains local, the port uses a thin center-expanding focus line, automatic ports display their real value without becoming fixed settings, and relay/pause buttons match backup-page spacing. ([#521](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/521)) - Improved: Phone wheel acknowledgements avoid serializing a full workspace snapshot, while continuous scrolling retains momentum and stops when input ownership, attachment or the active target changes. ([#498](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/498), [#519](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/519)) - Improved: Streaming emoji continuation state stays inline and is initialized only when needed; ordinary BMP text retains its original input path. Focused correctness and cost checks accompany the implementation. ([#451](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/451)) - Improved: Git and project metadata queries run in owned background processes or SSH channels with time/output limits, cancellation and connection-scoped caches. They do not inject discovery commands into the interactive terminal or reuse results from another connection. ([#467](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/467), [#468](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/468), [#470](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/470)) - Improved: WSL launch parsing, guest identity and directory inheritance now use shared rules. The proposed Zsh startup takeover and guest-shell probes were withdrawn from this release, preserving the guest default shell and existing startup ownership. ([#351](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/351)) - Improved: SSH forwarding review and regression coverage now includes native shortcut selection, dialog input hit targets and edited port values. Screenshot/probe adjustments were used for review and are not additional shipped UI features. ([#280](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/280)) - Improved: Native regression fixtures reveal controls before pointer input, retain their windows and rendering assets, use shared prompt/viewport helpers, preserve acquisition diagnostics, and isolate shared settings/Git resources. Coverage includes split duplication, startup directories, keyboard-layout switching, settings event routing and mobile scrolling. ([#476](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/476), [#495](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/495), [#496](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/496), [#508](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/508)) - Improved: PowerShell prompt regressions exercise Conda and real Python virtual environments, repeated wrapping and ordinary-function status behavior. The contributor-only diagnostic workflow was removed from the final release tree. ([#453](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/453)) - Improved: Theme-package, terminal-effect, completion, WSL and media documentation records the retained interfaces and verification boundaries. Theme-format diagnostics and test names are clearer; associated import/format corrections preserve behavior. ([#523](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/523)) - Improved: Release metadata follows the full history from v2.1.1 through the unpublished 2.1.2 installation candidate to v2.2.0, including the prior release checksum backfill, product/Android versions and bilingual notes. [Full commit comparison](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/compare/v2.1.1...v2.2.0) covers 133 non-merge commits and 124 integration merges; related iterations are consolidated here. ([#432](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/432), [#500](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/500)) - Improved: Tagged publication requires the complete five-platform native suite, package/runtime evidence and the complete verified asset set before exposing a public stable release.  ### Fixed  - Fixed: SSH port inputs render inside the dialog content area, accept pointer/keyboard editing and use the edited values; listener accept failures no longer permanently terminate forwarding. ([#280](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/280)) - Fixed: WSL-related splits, duplicated tabs, file/Git helpers and runtime execution retain the pane's actual distribution, explicit user and guest directory, including bare `wsl` launches after the system default changes. Parsing stops at guest-command boundaries and preserves the command's own arguments. ([#351](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/351)) - Fixed: WSL helpers use direct `--exec` argument boundaries, preserving quoted arguments and trailing backslashes. Unsupported quoted startup directories return a specific error instead of being rewritten or interpreted as shell commands. ([#351](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/351)) - Fixed: Theme-package fields have clear labels and a compact mode selector; editor actions remain reachable in narrow windows. ([#441](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/441)) - Fixed: Theme previews remain visible when scrolling theme choices and restore the selected appearance when transient preview ends. ([#443](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/443)) - Fixed: Backup-provider rows and wizard steps allow long titles and wrapped descriptions to remain visible in narrow layouts. ([#442](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/442)) - Fixed: Encrypted command backups include saved commands, and an open command manager refreshes after restoration or undo. The scope description is updated across the existing language catalogs. ([#448](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/448)) - Fixed: Closing the final tab preserves an enabled background-resident workspace and its consistent empty-window/session state. ([#446](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/446)) - Fixed: Streamed emoji components such as variation selectors, skin tones, regional indicators and joined sequences update the same terminal cluster with the correct cell width, wrapping and continuation behavior. ([#451](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/451)) - Fixed: Each PowerShell prompt wrapper keeps its original prompt owner, including virtual-environment activation/deactivation and repeated initialization. Only the outer wrapper publishes completion/status protocol markers, preserving original prompt effects and exit status. ([#453](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/453)) - Fixed: Duplicating a terminal tab reconstructs the complete nested split tree, split ratios and focused pane, preserving each pane's launch identity and directory while allocating independent pane/session identities. ([#455](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/455)) - Fixed: Configured startup directories apply both on cold start and when opening another window in a resident application, while explicit launch directory choices retain precedence. ([#485](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/485), [#492](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/492)) - Fixed: Owned Windows helper processes are attached to their cleanup job before they can run or spawn descendants, closing the fast-child race in cancellation and resource cleanup. ([#470](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/470)) - Fixed: The terminal paste menu displays its intended icon. ([#487](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/487)) - Fixed: Relay deployment reuses the shared SSH private-key reader and passphrase rules, and can add a saved key-authenticated host directly from the setup flow. ([#488](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/488), [#490](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/490)) - Fixed: Offline relay kits contain the installer and required packaging inputs; notices are collected only from resolved dependencies, and kit/APK evidence verifies matching native binaries, source identity and license content. ([#489](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/489)) - Fixed: Unchanged managed hook reconciliation preserves configuration bytes and hook positions, avoiding unnecessary rewrites while retaining edited-entry ownership checks and duplicate repair. ([#491](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/491)) - Fixed: Active-session probing resolves symbolic-link rollout directories before matching session paths. ([#497](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/497)) - Fixed: Phone terminal swipes reach the native wheel/input routing path. Momentum, gesture cancellation and target ownership behave consistently when detaching or switching terminals. ([#498](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/498)) - Fixed: Ordinary phone terminal history scrolls independently of the desktop viewport, with bounded paging, preserved anchors through reflow and stale-frame cancellation. Alternate-screen applications retain their native input behavior. ([#519](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/519)) - Fixed: Offline relay state no longer interferes with LAN pairing; refreshing or changing LAN addresses does not restart a healthy relay route. ([#522](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/522)) - Fixed: WSL native editor queries survive Win32 input mode, and valid visible completion candidates remain available during asynchronous directory refresh. ([#504](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/504)) - Fixed: Translated short-choice settings retain readable segmented capsules instead of falling back to dropdowns because of fixed widths; groups wrap together when needed. ([#505](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/505)) - Fixed: Restored PowerShell sessions refresh obsolete managed bootstrap references before launch. Managed scripts are identified by release and content while user-supplied launch commands retain their own settings. ([#514](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/514)) - Fixed: Windows day/night transitions capture composited client pixels; rapid reversal, resize, scale changes, focus loss and capture failure clear or replace temporary images within the existing resource limits. ([#506](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/506)) - Fixed: The background shader compiler resolves from its own source directory, avoiding the incorrect module path in the integrated build. ([#501](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/501)) - Fixed: Native CI applies the intended optimization profile to the media acceptance package, preserving media verification without the incorrect package override. ([#501](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/501)) - Fixed: Conformance launches isolate their runtime identity from the parent application and normalize temporary Windows paths so tests reach the intended instance. ([#507](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/507), [#508](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/508)) - Fixed: The source-name checker decodes added UTF-8 source independently of potentially truncated Git diff hunk headers, while still rejecting invalid added text and retaining its positive/negative fixtures. - Fixed: Release-pipeline follow-up: the Android archive builder is isolated from the enclosing application Git tag. The v2.2.0 APK and matching relay kit were rebuilt from the unchanged release source and passed 184 unit tests and 24 instrumented tests, with no skipped tests; the tag and desktop packages remain unchanged.  ## 中文  ### 新增  - 新增：与窗格会话绑定的 SSH 本地端口转发，提供 Ports 入口和本地/远端端口编辑对话框；仅监听本地回环地址，限制并发连接，接收连接出错后重试，并在所属会话退出时清理。 ([#280](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/280)) - 新增：已确认的本地 Windows 管理员会话显示静态盾牌，位于程序或 Agent 图标及标签名之前，尺寸与任务图标一致并带悬停说明；SSH 和 WSL 保持各自会话身份。对应 [#436](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/issues/436)。 ([#449](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/449)) - 新增：自定义主题的背景图片选择、不透明度、适配方式、对齐和窗口模糊控件，并提供跟随当前编辑草稿的实时预览。 ([#437](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/437)) - 新增：可携带原生主题 JSON、静态背景图、可选预览图、作者、许可证和版本信息的主题 ZIP；导入后添加到本地主题库，应用主题仍由用户单独操作。 ([#438](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/438)) - 新增：`pebrel theme pack`、`check`、`import`、`limits` 命令，提供 JSON 结果、对应退出码及 Bash/Zsh/Fish 补全；相对图片路径以主题 JSON 所在目录解析。 ([#440](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/440)) - 新增：主题编辑器内的 ZIP 导入、导出对话框，支持预先检查压缩包、填写包信息、确认操作，以及继续编辑已安装主题。 ([#441](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/441)) - 新增：Windows 视频及流式 GIF 背景，明确区分图片、视频和 GIF 来源；播放限制解码、帧和 GPU 资源，并随窗口可见性及活动状态调度。 ([#501](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/501)) - 新增：颗粒、霓虹漩涡、极光飘带、流动丝绸背景预设及本地 WGSL 背景文件；效果需要显式开启，仅保存文件路径不会启动执行。 ([#501](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/501)) - 新增：在具备对应原生能力的 Windows、Linux Vulkan 和 macOS Metal 窗口中运行有序 WGSL 终端后处理；支持最多八个文件、八个片段处理阶段，显式重新加载、独立启用及关闭/仅聚焦/始终动画策略。 ([#513](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/513)) - 新增：终端效果可读取可见终端纹理、视口、单调时间、焦点、当前/前一光标及解析后的 256 色调色板，并提供光标拖尾和扫描线/暗角示例；文字保持原生像素分辨率，补全和输入法预编辑层随后绘制。 ([#513](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/513)) - 新增：Windows 日夜主题切换的短时圆形揭示过渡，以及设置搜索框从中心展开的焦点反馈；减少动效时即时切换，客户区捕获未就绪时仍保持正常主题切换。 ([#506](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/506)) - 新增：依据 PSReadLine、Bash Readline、Zsh ZLE 的原生编辑缓冲和真实光标生成补全，并接入已声明能力的 WSL/SSH 启动环境；查询保留用户按键绑定，区分实际输入与 Shell 预测文字。 ([#469](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/469), [#473](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/473)) - 新增：按光标位置提供 Git 显式跟踪选项、已配置远端、分支、引用及 push/pull/fetch 参数角色补全，元数据来自当前选定的本地、WSL 或已认证 SSH 环境。 ([#465](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/465), [#466](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/466), [#467](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/467)) - 新增：按所选包提供 npm、pnpm、Yarn 工作区及脚本补全，支持工作区名称/路径、已支持的筛选器、依赖选择和对应包管理器的脚本集合；本地与远端工程共用选择规则。 ([#468](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/468)) - 新增：随 Android APK 提供同版本离线中转安装包与安装指引，包含 Linux x64/ARM64 程序、下载入口、产品及依赖许可证、源码身份和校验值验证。 ([#489](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/489)) - 新增：运行时与手机桥接的有界 `pane.scroll` 输入和分页 `screen_history` 读取，校验屏幕格坐标、携带历史身份，并同步更新协议 schema，支撑手机终端视口。 ([#498](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/498), [#519](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/519))  ### 改进  - 改进：主题预览复用已准备图片，丢弃过期加载，并在编辑器关闭时释放所属资源；文件选择和主题包结果绑定原草稿，导入导出在界面线程之外执行。 ([#437](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/437), [#441](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/441)) - 改进：主题包安装前校验实际字节限制、声明文件、资源哈希和可携带路径；静态图片导入导出共用规则，该包格式不激活或安装动画、视频及着色器。 ([#438](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/438), [#440](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/440), [#441](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/441)) - 改进：背景解码、窗口材质与终端效果控制器明确资源归属并共享资源预算；中途引入的重复原生视觉抽象已回退，最终保留的原生适配器沿用现有界面生命周期。 ([#501](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/501), [#513](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/513)) - 改进：效果可用性跟随当前窗口后端；不支持的窗口保留已保存来源的编辑与关闭操作，动画调度遵循焦点、可见性及显式播放策略。 ([#513](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/513)) - 改进：终端效果的文件顺序、重新加载和播放设置集中到“高级效果”；自定义背景控件按需显示，保留已保存的启用状态及播放选择。 ([#505](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/505)) - 改进：手机远程设置将网卡地址、端口和刷新操作放在同一行；下拉框单独响应悬停，端口聚焦时细线从中心展开，自动端口显示实际值但不保存成固定端口，配置中转和暂停按钮与备份页保持一致留白。 ([#521](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/521)) - 改进：手机滚轮回执避免序列化完整工作区快照；连续滚动保留惯性，并在输入归属、附着状态或活动目标改变时停止。 ([#498](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/498), [#519](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/519)) - 改进：流式 emoji 续写状态保持内联并按需初始化，普通 BMP 文本沿用原输入路径；实现配有针对性的正确性及成本核验。 ([#451](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/451)) - 改进：Git 和工程元数据查询通过所属后台进程或 SSH 通道执行，限制时间及输出，支持取消并按连接区分缓存；发现命令不注入交互终端，也不复用其他连接的结果。 ([#467](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/467), [#468](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/468), [#470](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/470)) - 改进：WSL 启动参数解析、来宾身份及目录继承统一使用共享规则；本轮曾加入的 Zsh 启动接管与来宾 Shell 探测已移出发行内容，保留来宾默认 Shell 和既有启动归属。 ([#351](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/351)) - 改进：SSH 转发的审阅与回归覆盖原生快捷键、对话框输入命中区域和修改后的端口值；截图及探针调整用于审阅，不另列为已交付界面功能。 ([#280](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/280)) - 改进：原生回归在指针操作前显示真实控件，保留测试窗口与绘制资源，复用提示符及视口工具，保留元数据获取诊断，并隔离共享设置/Git 资源；覆盖分屏复制、启动目录、键盘布局切换、设置事件路由及手机滚动。 ([#476](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/476), [#495](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/495), [#496](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/496), [#508](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/508)) - 改进：PowerShell 提示符回归覆盖 Conda、真实 Python 虚拟环境、重复包装及普通函数的状态语义；仅用于贡献者排查的诊断工作流已从最终发行树移除。 ([#453](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/453)) - 改进：主题包、终端效果、补全、WSL 和媒体文档记录最终保留的接口及验证边界；主题格式诊断和测试命名更明确，配套导入顺序及格式修正保留原行为。 ([#523](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/523)) - 改进：发布元数据覆盖 v2.1.1 经未公开的 2.1.2 安装候选到 v2.2.0 的完整历史，包括上一版校验值回填、产品/Android 版本及双语说明；[完整提交对比](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/compare/v2.1.1...v2.2.0) 包含 133 个非合并提交与 124 个整合提交，相关迭代在本说明中合并表述。 ([#432](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/432), [#500](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/500)) - 改进：标签发布在公开稳定版之前要求五平台完整原生测试、打包与运行证据，以及完整资产集合校验全部通过。  ### 修复  - 修复：SSH 端口输入框正确绘制在对话框内容区，支持鼠标和键盘编辑并使用修改后的值；监听器接收连接失败后不再永久停止转发。 ([#280](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/280)) - 修复：WSL 分屏、标签复制、文件/Git 辅助命令及运行时执行保留窗格实际使用的发行版、显式用户和来宾目录，包括系统默认发行版改变后的裸 `wsl` 启动；参数解析在来宾命令边界停止，保留命令自己的参数。 ([#351](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/351)) - 修复：WSL 辅助命令使用直接 `--exec` 参数边界，保留参数内部引号和末尾反斜杠；不支持的含引号启动目录返回明确错误，避免改写目录或将其解释为 Shell 命令。 ([#351](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/351)) - 修复：主题包字段补齐清晰标签并保持紧凑模式选择器，主题编辑操作在窄窗口中仍可访问。 ([#441](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/441)) - 修复：滚动主题选项时预览保持可见，临时预览结束后恢复选定外观。 ([#443](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/443)) - 修复：备份服务行和向导步骤在窄布局下保留长标题及换行说明，避免内容被压缩或遮挡。 ([#442](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/442)) - 修复：加密命令备份包含已保存命令，恢复或撤销后已打开的命令管理器同步刷新，并更新现有语言目录中的备份范围说明。 ([#448](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/448)) - 修复：关闭最后一个标签后保留已启用的后台驻留工作区，并保持空窗口及会话状态一致。 ([#446](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/446)) - 修复：流式输入中的变体选择符、肤色、区域指示符及连接序列更新同一 emoji 字素簇，正确处理终端格宽、换行和续写。 ([#451](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/451)) - 修复：每层 PowerShell 提示符包装保留自己的原始提示符归属，覆盖虚拟环境激活/退出和重复初始化；仅最外层发送补全及状态协议标记，保留原提示符副作用和退出状态。 ([#453](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/453)) - 修复：复制终端标签时重建完整嵌套分屏树、分屏比例和焦点窗格，保留各窗格启动身份及目录，并为副本分配独立窗格和会话身份。 ([#455](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/455)) - 修复：配置的启动目录同时用于冷启动和驻留应用再次开窗，显式指定的启动目录仍保持优先级。 ([#485](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/485), [#492](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/492)) - 修复：Windows 辅助子进程在运行或创建后代前先加入所属清理作业，修复快速子进程导致取消和资源清理失去归属的竞态。 ([#470](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/470)) - 修复：终端粘贴菜单缺少图标的问题。 ([#487](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/487)) - 修复：中转部署复用共享 SSH 私钥读取器及口令规则，并支持在配置流程中直接添加使用密钥认证的已保存主机。 ([#488](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/488), [#490](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/490)) - 修复：离线中转包补齐安装器及必需打包输入，仅收集实际解析依赖的许可证，并通过安装包/APK 证据核对原生程序、源码身份和许可证内容。 ([#489](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/489)) - 修复：托管 hook 内容未变化时保留配置原始字节及 hook 位置，避免无谓改写，同时保留被编辑条目的归属检查和重复条目修复。 ([#491](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/491)) - 修复：活动会话探测在匹配会话路径前解析符号链接形式的 rollout 目录。 ([#497](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/497)) - 修复：手机终端滑动接入原生滚轮及输入路由，脱离或切换终端时一致处理惯性、手势取消和目标归属。 ([#498](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/498)) - 修复：手机普通终端历史独立于桌面视口滚动，采用有界分页，在重排后保留阅读锚点并取消过期帧；备用屏幕应用保留原生输入行为。 ([#519](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/519)) - 修复：中转离线状态不再干扰局域网配对，刷新或更改局域网地址不会重启健康的中转连接。 ([#522](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/522)) - 修复：WSL 原生编辑器查询在 Win32 输入模式下不再丢失，异步目录刷新期间保留有效的可见补全候选。 ([#504](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/504)) - 修复：翻译后的短选项不再因固定宽度退回下拉框，分段胶囊保持可读，并在空间不足时整组换行。 ([#505](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/505)) - 修复：恢复的 PowerShell 会话在启动前刷新过期托管脚本引用；托管脚本按发行版本及内容标识，用户自定义启动命令保留自身设置。 ([#514](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/514)) - 修复：Windows 日夜切换捕获已合成的客户区像素；快速反向切换、缩放、比例变化、失焦或捕获失败时，在现有资源限制内清理或替换临时画面。 ([#506](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/506)) - 修复：背景着色器编译模块从自身源码目录解析，修复整合构建中的模块路径错误。 ([#501](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/501)) - 修复：原生 CI 为媒体验收包应用正确的优化配置，修复错误包级覆盖并保留媒体验证。 ([#501](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/501)) - 修复：运行验收启动隔离父应用的运行时身份，并规范化 Windows 临时路径，使测试指向预期实例。 ([#507](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/507), [#508](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/508)) - 修复：源码名称检查独立解码新增 UTF-8 内容，避免 Git diff hunk 标题在多字节边界截断导致误报，同时保留非法新增文本检查及正反用例。 - 修复：发布流程后续修正：Android 解压源码构建与外层应用 Git 标签隔离；v2.2.0 APK 和配套中转包仍从原发布源码构建，184 项单测、24 项模拟器测试通过且无跳过，版本标签及桌面包保持不变。  ## Contributors  <p>   <a href="https://github.com/WilliamWang1721"><img src="https://github.com/WilliamWang1721.png?size=96" width="48" height="48" alt="WilliamWang1721" title="WilliamWang1721" /></a>   <a href="https://github.com/MomentDerek"><img src="https://github.com/MomentDerek.png?size=96" width="48" height="48" alt="MomentDerek" title="MomentDerek" /></a>   <a href="https://github.com/azzliang6"><img src="https://github.com/azzliang6.png?size=96" width="48" height="48" alt="azzliang6" title="azzliang6" /></a>   <a href="https://github.com/Kuddev"><img src="https://github.com/Kuddev.png?size=96" width="48" height="48" alt="Kuddev" title="Kuddev" /></a> </p>  [@WilliamWang1721](https://github.com/WilliamWang1721) · [@MomentDerek](https://github.com/MomentDerek) · [@azzliang6](https://github.com/azzliang6) · [@Kuddev](https://github.com/Kuddev)  Thank you for the 47 PRs included in this release. PR links appear beside the corresponding changes above. 感谢以上贡献者参与本次发行纳入的 47 个 PR，完整 PR 链接已标在上方对应改动后。  ---  **SHA256**  - `Pebrel-v2.2.0-linux-x64-preview.AppImage`: `2b32cf9d4409bc70d843b6408348aaded2c2ec9deff6f69f6987575331771523` - `Pebrel-v2.2.0-linux-x64-preview.deb`: `f311062bc18c95f9386f92a8781f8724fbb062ed8f0f26793be47d640bfc11f3` - `Pebrel-v2.2.0-linux-x64-preview.tar.gz`: `fdcfec88cc917bfa74cba453815112eb82a1bbd150a596570ed0953d8b7d1c9d` - `Pebrel-v2.2.0-macos-arm64-preview.dmg`: `29dcdafb761d742e25221dd9589377e8646bc2511923da1ffde9ef47aceef224` - `Pebrel-v2.2.0-macos-x64-preview.dmg`: `01e1af358ed38f840fd72a253e12be82f0f25fbeb20e52b2a6c64488b36e83c2` - `Pebrel-v2.2.0-windows-x64.zip`: `20023c035d7aa28680345c0049781b03a0c6a430cfdf5ebf8b3bd3d04ebf4c62` - `Pebrel-v2.2.0-windows-x64-setup.exe`: `6d9becba6a171bcf405f93c05dd73336a2a88079e5baaf643071646966a726bf` - `Pebrel-v2.2.0-windows-arm64.zip`: `780fa61cd4260b6e8a1be9ac4e6e030bcc5df90ebcb4dc9f429551c0255c5a8b` - `Pebrel-v2.2.0-windows-arm64-setup.exe`: `2468bbad526938d9e0060a37dd81756107e4ed0c3d96056164fe455e0711f92f` - `Pebrel-v2.2.0-android-universal-preview.apk`: `9468f97673f9cb9c853e10841740ec04fa228e90bba3ce19ba72b65fe7c94f5e` - `Pebrel-v2.2.0-relay-manual.tar.gz`: `cb4b5cb08fd35054c1be2ade7afe236d612924f2bcb4f24bb94ca3abd899b06a`

### 2026-10-13T17:26:40Z [**farlume/termish**](https://github.com/farlume/termish)

```
### 修复  - 修复应用光标模式下方向键及 Home/End 的转义序列，避免输入 A/B/C/D 等字母。 - 工具栏在按键发送时读取当前会话的光标模式，避免继续使用界面重组前的旧状态。  ### 验证  两种光标模式、功能键、分片模式更新、会话隔离及后台恢复的单元回归通过；Android/iOS 共享代码构建、本地 lint 和签名包校验通过。真实设备上的间歇性复现，以及真实 SSH 切 tab／前后台端到端验收仍待复核。  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/farlume/termish/compare/v1.8.9...v1.8.10

### 2026-10-13T14:11:44Z [**ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI**](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI)

```
自 v0.14.0 以来，dsh-TUI 有了自己的启动入口，带来重做的模型选择器、终端原生光标动画、任务完成通知、侧问面板改版和跨内核会话预加载。本版同时扩展插件查询命令、发布第一版冻结的插件契约，并补齐启动页图片输入、侧栏拖拽、樱花粉主题、权限记忆与上游断链重试。  ## 升级与行为变化  - **插件命令改名**：`/plugins` 改为 `/plugin`，本版已移除旧命令，**没有保留弃用别名**。手动命令、快捷调用和自动化请改用 `/plugin`，例如 `/plugins check …` 改为 `/plugin check …`。（#1504 by @Lucas2011wastaken） - **TUI 设置迁移**：`/settings` 的 `dsh-tui` 分区改存 `~/.dsh-tui/settings.json`，DSH、Claude、Codex 共用。首次启动自动导入 profile 补丁和旧 `~/.dsh/settings.yaml` 中的可编辑字段；`!!js` 表达式不导入。旧 profile 中残留的字段仍可能在 DSH 内核下提供部署默认值，建议迁移后清理重复配置。（#1441 by @MomentDerek；#1483 by @ccch1mneyyy） - **启动与恢复更严格**：默认从本包入口启动；`DSH_TUI_HOST_ENTRY=0` 可恢复原启动方式，DSH 宿主模块不可用时自动回退并说明原因。裸 `--resume`、`-c`、`--continue` 的恢复目标必须属于最终所选后端；没有可恢复会话或后端身份不匹配时明确报错并非零退出。安全模式的“重试正常启动”仍保留告警后冷启动的退路。（#1441 by @MomentDerek；#1449 by @Lucas2011wastaken） - **默认选择优先沿用上次使用**：DSH 的模型、preset 和推理强度持久化选择现在优先于静态部署配置；恢复会话继续采用自身记录的模型路由。`DSH_TUI_PRESET` 仍可指定本次启动的 preset，`/settings` 的显式 `effortDefault` 仍优先于上次推理档位。（#1430 by @ccch1mneyyy） - **官方请求元数据恢复默认**：TUI profile 继承 DSH 官方的插件包清单、增量会话日志与反馈遥测配置。前两项随 `deepseek-official` 请求发送到配置的 `baseURL`，包括第三方网关；反馈 OTel 只在明确反馈后上传。需要关闭请求元数据时，在 profile 中分别设置 `plugin-package-inventory-deepseek.enabled: false` 和 `session-log-deepseek.enabled: false`；`DSH_TELEMETRY_MODE=DISABLED` 只控制 OTel，不控制这两项请求元数据。（#1375 by @ccch1mneyyy）  ## 新功能与体验  ### 启动、模型与会话  - **界面先出现，后端随后加载**：`dsh-tui` 先挂载可交互界面，再在同一进程加载 DSH；Claude/Codex 组合本包与 profile 声明的第三方插件，不加载完整 `dsh-base`。加载期间就能输入草稿、使用可用的本地命令，主题和面板等插件贡献随后加入。（#1441 by @MomentDerek） - **三个内核共用本地设置**：切换内核后，语言、布局和其他 TUI 设置使用同一份 `settings.json`。切换到另一 profile 时，其部署配置补充在用户显式设置之下；设置文件损坏会在 `/settings` 显示提示，覆盖前保留原文件副本。（#1441 by @MomentDerek；#1483 by @ccch1mneyyy） - **内核直达命令**：新增 `/dsh`、`/claude`、`/codex`，记住选择并重启进入目标内核的新会话。选择当前内核保留本会话，回合运行中拒绝切换；未安装 Claude 时可进入安装向导，Codex 缺失或版本过旧时给出对应提示。（#1497 by @CikeSeven） - **模型与推理等级同页选择**：`/model` 重做为提供商标签、模型列表和推理档位三个区域。DSH 的“最近使用”排在首位，当前模型去重置顶，`Tab` / `Shift+Tab` 切提供商；Claude/Codex 直接展示自身模型目录。`↑/↓` 选模型，`←/→` 调推理等级，`Enter` 应用、`Esc` 取消；点击推理档位会直接应用当前模型和强度。短终端优先保留聚焦模型与确认入口。（#1436、#1466 by @CikeSeven） - **DSH 真正的同会话模型切换**：`/model` 直接切换后续请求的路由，保留会话 ID、历史、权限和草稿，避免复制历史产生同名会话链。停放后重新挂载仍保留选择，下一次请求记录实际模型及变化提示；DSH 低于 `0.1.2-alpha.3` 时明确提示升级。（#1275 by @xuzijian2019） - **三个内核的会话列表都支持首屏快照**：DSH、Claude、Codex 重开后先显示上次完整读取的会话列表，快照按后端与存储目录隔离，后台继续核对变化。（#1458 by @ccch1mneyyy） - **当前工作区优先加载与启动预热**：启动时预加载历史，打开 `/resume` 复用在途读取并接收已有结果。三个后端先显示当前工作区，再补其他目录；Codex 复用已启动连接，数据库快读与常规首批查询并行，新会话和标题增量显示。后台更新保持用户已手动选择的行。（#1464、#1494 by @CikeSeven） - **会话管理默认选中最近会话**：打开 `/resume` 后可直接 `Enter` 恢复当前工作区最近使用的会话；切栏或鼠标移入列表保留选择，换工作区时选中该目录最新会话，没有历史时才选“新建会话”。（#1423、#1468 by @CikeSeven） - **启动页可关闭**：`/settings` 新增 `launchpad` 开关，默认开启；关闭后普通启动直接进入聊天页，重启生效。指定恢复会话或首句的启动继续直达对话。（#1476 by @ccch1mneyyy） - **启动页支持图片附件**：剪贴板图片和粘贴的图片路径可暂存为 `[Image #N]`，随首条消息发送；图片标记支持整体移动与删除。命令不接受图片时保留草稿及附件，后端加载完成后也保留文字、光标位置和图片。加载期间提前按 `Enter` 不自动排队发送，就绪后再次确认即可。（#1460、#1492 by @CikeSeven）  ### 终端交互、侧栏与外观  - **原生光标动画与拖尾**：输入框继承终端配置的光标形状、颜色、闪烁、动画和拖尾，inline 与 fullscreen 均支持。模型选择器和会话管理界面导航时，原生光标跟随聚焦标签、模型、档位、工作区或会话行，停稳 0.5 秒后隐藏；文本输入光标持续可见。动画与拖尾由终端自身设置开启。（#1428、#1443、#1468 by @CikeSeven） - **复制后保留选区，拖选时光标跟随**：全屏拖选自动复制后继续显示选区，`Esc` 取消或重新拖选替换；高亮跳过行尾填充和空白行，保留代码缩进。拖选时原生光标跟随实际文字边缘，松手后回到输入框。浮层优先处理 `Esc`，取消正文选区的那次按键不会打断生成。（#1440 by @CikeSeven） - **侧栏分隔线可拖拽**：全屏分栏时左右拖拽分隔线即可实时调宽，聊天列至少 64 列、侧栏至少 28 列；调宽保留焦点和未发送草稿，比例作用于当前会话。（#1391 by @CikeSeven） - **面板标签显示实际图标**：未选中面板改用各自注册的图标，内置和插件面板都适用；悬停显示名称，未读状态通过颜色、加粗和计数呈现。（#1393 by @CikeSeven） - **`/btw` 侧问面板改版**：聚焦侧栏后默认进入阅读层，`←/→` 切面板、`↑/↓` 和翻页键滚动；`Enter`、`Tab` 或点击输入框进入编辑层。新增带边框与焦点高亮的输入框、线程状态徽章、轮次分隔线和空态说明；空线程隐藏多余头部，全屏侧问场景仍默认进入编辑。中文 IME 的预编辑位置也跟随侧问输入框。（#1417、#1431 by @ccch1mneyyy） - **樱花粉主题家族**：新增深色 `pink-night`、浅色 `pink-day` 和 16 色回退 `pink-ansi`，通过 `/theme` 显式选择。主题选择器适配短终端，焦点行保持可见；品牌主题按实际色板明暗选择对应版本。（#1294 by @xiaoxiaohaigui） - **用户消息配色随内核区分**：雾蓝主题下使用陶土红用户标签，Claude 使用雾蓝，Codex 使用金色，浅色版同步调整；用户行的排版和加粗保持原有行为。（#1420 by @ccch1mneyyy） - **上下文条显示更准确的内容构成**：接入 DSH `contextBreakdown`，按后端占用量分配系统、提示、回复、思考和工具的分段及悬停明细；没有分类时显示实测已用量。分类仍是估算，不能当作分项账单。（#1351 by @xiaoxiaohaigui） - **长目标可读全文**：fullscreen/分栏下悬停被截断的目标正文可看全文；裸 `/goal` 把目标、状态、阶段、轮次或预算及可用命令打印到转录中，inline 也可阅读。超长单行继续使用转录既有的折叠与展开机制。（#1223 by @xuzijian2019）  ### 通知、权限、工具与插件  - **任务完成通知**：DSH、Claude、Codex 的实时主任务成功结束，且没有排队输入或活动目标时提醒。默认仅在终端失焦时通知；`/settings` 的 `taskNotifications` 可选 `unfocused`、`always`、`off`，`notificationMethod` 可选 `auto`、`osc9`、`bel`。通知带本地化标题和最新回复的单行纯文本摘要；自动适配 Ghostty/WezTerm、Kitty、iTerm2/Warp，其他终端响铃。tmux 需要开启 `focus-events` 与 `allow-passthrough`；不支持焦点报告时可选 `always`。回放、取消和失败不通知。（#1517 by @CikeSeven） - **DSH 权限选择跨会话记忆**：`/permission`、Shift+Tab 静态模式和官方命令完成的持久切换写入 `~/.dsh-tui/permission.json`，新会话经官方路径应用上次选择；临时计划模式不记入，显式 `DSH_PERMISSION_MODE` 优先，已不在名册中的权限身份跳过。（#1415 by @ccch1mneyyy） - **DSH 上游断链自动重试**：默认给当前实际使用且没有显式 `retryPolicy` 的 `llm-pi-ai` 渠道补充重试策略，覆盖 `STREAM_CLOSED` 等暂时性失败，首次请求后最多再重试 5 次。已有策略保持有效，闲置渠道不写入；`upstreamRetry: false` 可关闭策略播种。（#1415 by @ccch1mneyyy） - **Windows 全功能 `winbash`**：随包提供可手动挂到 profile host 层的 Git Bash 工具，对任意 Agent preset 生效。支持 `run_in_background`、`job_output` / `job_kill`，前台超时可转后台继续运行；缺少 job registry 时降级为前台执行。它需要按配置文档挂载，并与梁神模式内置的同名 `bash` 工具择一启用。（#1376 by @ccch1mneyyy） - **插件列表、详情和安装源查询**：DSH 下新增 `/plugin list`，简洁显示 profile 额外安装的插件；`/plugin info <名字或 spec>` 查询已装条目或待装包，`/plugin registries` 查看安装源，`/plugin check <路径>` 校验清单。子命令与含 `@scope/` 的已装包名支持补全，无参保留完整诊断报告；本版这些命令用于查询与校验，安装、卸载和启停动作尚未接入。（#1504 by @Lucas2011wastaken）  ## 插件与后端开发  - **后端注册与安装配方声明**：内置后端通过 `manifest.ts` 与生成索引进入运行时注册表，模型、内核目录及资源清理按声明接入；安装向导按后端声明的包名、版本和执行器定位目标。关闭向导会取消在途安装，确认面板显示实际安装包。（#1380、#1408 by @Lucas2011wastaken） - **新增 `Backend` 贡献族与准入链**：提供 `BackendSpec`、`validateBackendSpec`、`backendAdmission` 等公开声明与校验面，能力和权限声明进入准入判定，`Backend` 坐标接入 registry 与 host descriptor。Claude、Codex 已使用公开接缝；第三方后端安装后自动出现在 kernel picker 的完整装配链路仍未交付。（#1449、#1471、#1480、#1500 by @Lucas2011wastaken） - **第一版插件 API 形态冻结**：`tui.dsh/v1alpha1` 明确冻结公开面，设置区块、全屏场景、托管对话框、状态行、快捷键和条目渲染器纳入承诺：已发布形状只增不删、语义保持，破坏性变化使用新坐标，并先经过一个已发布次版本的弃用窗口。`Backend` 验收进入构建门禁；toast 与侧栏 Panel 继续处于实验阶段。（#1477、#1502 by @Lucas2011wastaken）  ## 修复与稳定性  - **启动草稿与命令补全**：自动接入后端时保留草稿与附件；启动页 `Tab` 补全、`Enter` 执行后清空，与聊天页一致。窄卡片中的补全面板不再溢出或产生幽灵空行；启动页的 `/resume`、`/home`、`/jobs` 正确打开整屏视图。（#1492、#1425、#1413 by @CikeSeven；#1344 by @xiaoxiaohaigui） - **剪贴板位图与 HiDPI 图片**：BMP/DIB 先通过现有纯 JS 解码器转 PNG 再进入附件管线，修复合法剪贴板位图被拒绝；Kitty 立绘按预留格子的物理像素缩放，在 HiDPI 终端中尺寸正确，并受单图与整帧预算约束。（#1490 by @ccch1mneyyy；#1498 by @CikeSeven） - **内核切换后的图片探测**：交接前停止旧进程预读 stdin，新进程能收到图片协议应答，立绘无需额外按键或鼠标事件即可恢复；退出和交接后停止迟到的迁移提示，避免旧进程崩溃。（#1509、#1438 by @CikeSeven） - **流式阅读与换行**：流式更新保留消息行的实测高度，向上阅读时保持手动滚动锚点；正文、任务卡、todo、宠物气泡、表格和行内公式恰好满行时不再多出续行分隔空格，复制保留原文分隔。（#1296 by @xiaoxiaohaigui；#1279、#1290 by @xuzijian2019） - **空会话不再污染历史**：DSH JSONL 下，启动或 `/new` 的权限初始化事件先留在内存，后续真实会话活动才保存完整日志；只有初始化的会话不会因 flush 落盘，`dsh web` 侧栏不再积累“未命名”空行。（#1395 by @CikeSeven；#1407 by @baobaolaodie） - **前台 Shell 不再留下重复任务卡**：前台 Bash 调用完成并由内核移除 job 后，转录不会残留同命令、同输出的后台任务卡，也不会误报“后台任务完成”。（#1430 by @ccch1mneyyy） - **Codex 评审、队列与侧问**：`/review` 完成或 Esc 取消后释放输入队列，清理命令预览，排队消息可用 `Alt+↑` 取回；新 thread 上的 `/btw` 临时只读 fork 正常工作。（#1507 by @CikeSeven；#1421 by @ccch1mneyyy） - **Codex provider 凭据桥接**：未在 shell 导出的 `env_key` 可从活动 DSH home 的凭据库读取，并回退默认 `~/.dsh`；仍找不到时启动提示原因和变量名，由 Codex 在实际请求时处理认证。（#1421、#1433 by @ccch1mneyyy） - **Claude CLI 探测与内核可用性**：识别 PATH 之外的 `~/.local/bin` 原生安装目录，并跟随 npm shim 找到真实脚本；SDK 缺失时显示发现的 CLI。已安装内核不再因检测不到凭据而被置灰，凭据也可来自 CLI 设置、helper 或渠道档案。（#1512 by @baobaolaodie；#1400 by @ccch1mneyyy） - **中文 Markdown 与子代理标题**：`**标签：**正文` 等紧邻中文标点的粗体正确闭合；并发派发和迟到启动的子代理卡片标题正确匹配任务身份。（#1385 by @ccch1mneyyy；#1389 by @MomentDerek） - **计划开关与启动排版**：`/plan on`、`/plan off` 只切换计划状态，不再把 `on/off` 作为提示词发送；收紧 ANSI Shadow 字距，并按品牌与节日标题预算启动页，保住窄屏输入区域。（#1372 by @Lucas2011wastaken；#1411 by @ccch1mneyyy）  ## 安装与更新  sh npm install -g @deepseek-ai/dsh @deepseek-harness-tui/dsh-tui@0.15.0   已安装用户可使用 `/update` 或 `dsh-tui update`。Release 资产提供一键安装整合包、Linux/macOS/Windows 便携包及 `SHA256SUMS`。  <!-- dsh-tui:generated-notes --> <!-- Release notes generated using configuration in .github/release.yml at v0.15.0 -->  ## What's Changed * chore(release): prepare v0.14.1 by @CikeSeven in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1367 * revert(release): **moke · 墨客 v0.1.21** — Android 原生 SSH / mosh 终端。   ### 新增 - **终端里的链接可以点了**：单击终端输出里的 http / https 链接，底部弹出操作条，可以用浏览器打开或复制。被折行拆开的长链接（上千字符的下载或回调链接也一样）、紧跟中文标点的链接都能完整识别；操作条最多显示三行，过长时省略中段、保留结尾，复制和打开用的始终是完整链接。点在别处照旧弹出键盘；远端程序（如 vim、tmux）自己接收鼠标点击时不受影响。本机地址（`localhost` 等）提供「转发并打开」。 - **端口转发**：把远端机器上的端口转到手机本机，用浏览器访问没有公网地址的服务（例如远端的 dev server）。入口在终端 ⋮「端口转发」；有转发在运行时顶栏出现转发图标。点终端里的 `localhost` 链接可以一步「转发并打开」。主机编辑页可以填「连接后自动转发的端口」。只监听手机本机，局域网里的其他设备访问不到；SSH 与 mosh 会话都支持。远端服务还没起来时打开一次失败，转发不受影响，服务起来后刷新即可。 - **响铃与通知提醒**（设置 → 终端与输入，默认关闭）：远端程序响铃，或发出 OSC 9 / OSC 777 通知序列时，如果你不在这个会话里，用系统通知提醒你；点通知直接回到该会话。 - **底部快捷键可以更换**。设置 → 终端与输入 → 底部快捷键，点一格换成别的键。每排末尾的「更多」和「文本」保持不动，也可以恢复默认。默认键被换掉后会出现在「更多」面板最上面，随时还能按到。  ### 修复 - **首次连接时显示的主机指纹现在与 `ssh-keygen -lf` 一致**。此前确认弹窗让你在服务器上执行 `ssh-keygen -lf` 核对，显示的却是按另一种编码算出的值，两者永远对不上。已保存的旧记录会在下次连接时自动换成新格式，无需任何操作；换过密钥的主机照旧拒绝连接。服务器使用主机证书时，按证书里的主机公钥核对，证书重签不再被当成密钥变更。 - **连接失败不再显示成「会话已结束」**：地址、网络、认证或主机密钥出问题时，底部改为「连接失败」，并提供「编辑」直接修改这台主机的配置。连上之后才断开的，终端里写「SSH 连接中断」，不再误称「连接失败」。 - **关闭 mosh 会话后不再在服务器上留下一条 SSH 连接**。 - **「无法滚动」提示不再提供「改用方向键滚动」**。那个按钮会把全局设置改成方向键，之后在 mosh 的命令提示符下一滑就翻命令历史，提示也不再出现。已经点过的，可以在 设置 → 终端与输入 → 全屏程序内滑动 改回「智能」。 - **Ctrl / Alt 不再残留到下一次输入**：点了 Ctrl 再按退格、回车或方向键后，Ctrl 会熄灭；离开会话再回来、或隐藏底部快捷键时，亮着的修饰键会一并取消，不会在看不见的情况下作用到下一个字。 - **返回键先收起链接操作条和「更多」面板**，再离开终端。「更多」面板开着时点链接，操作条显示在面板之上。 - **点后台保活通知会回到会话**：只有一个会话时直接进入它，有多个时进入会话列表。此前只会把应用拉到前台。  ### 优化 - **终端顶栏收成一行**。会话名、协议和延迟排在同一行；返回、转发、tmux 和菜单仍在这一行上。 - **Ctrl、Alt、Shift 按住即锁定**。点一下只对下一次按键生效，再点一下取消；按住则一直生效，直到再点一下。按住要多久跟系统的「触摸和按住延迟」一致。 - 终端 ⋮ 里的「字号」与「恢复默认字号」合并为一行。 - **危险操作统一二次确认**：删除主机、删除字体、清除已保存的主机指纹、清理已结束会话、恢复默认外观，执行前都会说明将失去什么并请你确认。删除主机此前是一点即删、连同密码 / 私钥与指纹一起丢失。   ### 安装（两个包择一） - `moke-v0.1.21.apk` — 常规包，中文回退用内置思源黑体子集，体积小。 - `moke-v0.1.21-maple.apk` — 自带 Maple Mono 并设为默认中文回退（开箱中英等宽），包更大。  允许"安装未知来源应用"后安装（稳定签名，可覆盖升级）。  ### 特性 - 多会话，原生终端渲染（Apache-2.0 terminal-view 内核） - SSH（密码 / 私钥）+ mosh（UDP 漫游） - 实时预览外观 · 多套配色 · 字体管理（主字体 + 中文回退） - 连接分组排序 · 双排附加键 · 文本段输入  本版改动见 [CHANGELOG](https://github.com/briqt/moke/blob/main/CHANGELOG.md)。   **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/briqt/moke/compare/v0.1.20...v0.1.21 
```

### 2026-10-13T15:41:22Z [**herdrdev/herdr**](https://github.com/herdrdev/herdr)

```
### Added - `herdr plugin update` updates plugins installed from GitHub, all at once or by name. Running plugin panes keep working on their old files, and new runs pick up the update without a config reload. Locally linked plugins are left alone. (#4245, thanks @JJLiebig) - Windows notifications stay in Notification Center. Clicking one jumps to its pane and brings the terminal window forward. (#4773, #4803, thanks @JJLiebig)  ### Changed - An elevated Windows server now only accepts administrator clients by default. To let normal clients from the same account in, set `[server] allow_unelevated_clients = true` or start the server with `--allow-unelevated-clients`. Permission errors now say how to fix them, and sessions you can't reach show as unavailable instead of stopped. (#4717, #4769, #4777, #4779, thanks @JJLiebig) - On Linux and macOS, closing the shell that started Herdr no longer stops the server. Scripts that stopped Herdr with `SIGHUP` should use `herdr server stop` or `SIGTERM`. The server log now says why the server stopped. (#4920, #4965) - With mouse capture on, plugins that handle a link get first pick when you click it. Turn capture off to use your terminal's own link shortcuts, like Command-click on macOS. (#4874, #5064, thanks @JJLiebig) - Antigravity and Grok count as idle once they are back at their prompt, even if their own background tasks or subagents are still running. (#3530, #4735, #4959) - Ctrl+Shift+letter reaches plain shells as the Ctrl key, like in Alacritty and WezTerm. Super, Hyper and Meta shortcuts no longer type escape-code junk into apps that can't read them. (#4356, #5017)  ### Fixed - Restarting a Mac or shutting down Windows keeps your agents' sessions. Herdr saves before the system starts closing apps, so agents come back where they were. (#4414, #5013, #5057, #5092, thanks @JJLiebig) - Session saves are written to disk before they replace the old file, so a crash or power cut can't leave an unreadable save. (#5150, #5152, thanks @JJLiebig) - The server keeps running when the machine runs out of processes or threads, instead of taking every pane down with it. (#3324, #4958) - An out-of-range workspace number returns `workspace_not_found` instead of crashing the server. (#5096, #5171) - Suspending an agent with Ctrl+Z and bringing it back with `fg` keeps its name and saved conversation. (#1647, #4964) - Restoring a session stays responsive when a checkout's Git data is slow or missing. Worktree groups survive checkouts that are temporarily unavailable, and opening the same repository twice no longer nests one workspace inside the other. (#4926, #4736, #4945, #4770, #4772, thanks @minatoaquaMK2 and @JJLiebig) - Closing a workspace's only pane asks first, like closing its last tab already does. Turn this off with `ui.confirm_close = false`. (#5143, #5173) - Ctrl shortcuts like Ctrl+W work on non-Latin keyboard layouts such as Russian, Greek or Hebrew. Latin layouts and AltGr characters keep their normal meaning. (#1079, #4961) - Accent keys and AltGr shortcuts work on Windows. (#4688, #5021, thanks @JJLiebig) - Shift+Enter and other enhanced keys keep working after the outer terminal resets, for example after VS Code's Reload Window. (#4985, #5088) - Quick Escape taps work on macOS with WezTerm's Kitty keyboard mode. (#1266, #5089) - Kitty keyboard apps get key releases and F1, F2 and F4 correctly, and Backspace works when the terminal sends Ctrl+H for it. (#4184, #4403, #3244, #5017) - Keyboard and terminal color replies no longer leak stray characters into pane input, and a half-finished color reply no longer freezes typing. (#4856, #4025, #5052, #5017, #5087) - Fish no longer repeats prompt fragments while you resize a pane. The same problem with zsh and Powerlevel10k is still open, waiting on a Ghostty fix. (#3672, #4977) - Apps that ask for pixel mouse coordinates get them, and clicks land in the right place on terminals that only report cells. (#4750, #3675, #5017, #4971) - Turning off Kitty graphics makes panes stop advertising image support, so apps fall back to text. (#4978, #4979) - Typing shows up faster, especially in PowerShell. (#4884, #5149, thanks @JJLiebig) - Big sessions use less CPU on busy agents and idle shells, hidden panes use less memory, and Windows panes need fewer threads. (#4506, #5083, #4845, #4847, #4849, #4850, #4873, #5085, #5091, thanks @JJLiebig) - New workspaces, tabs and splits start their shell at the right size instead of borrowing another pane's size. (#4419, #4873) - Forwarded SSH agents are no longer contacted every second, which could eventually break SSH and Git authentication. (#4931, #4962) - Sidebar width, collapsed groups and agent sorting on remote machines survive reconnects. Switching workspaces quickly while changing machines no longer gets canceled. (#4364, #5019, #4536, #5061, thanks @minatoaquaMK2 and @JJLiebig) - The sidebar collapse button stays clickable when the agent list has a scrollbar. (#4692, #4734, thanks @minatoaquaMK2) - The welcome screen shows your configured prefix. (#4745, #4747, thanks @JJLiebig) - Ctrl+[ leaves workspace navigation. (#1431, #2340, thanks @qaz74107410) - Keybindings that use `+` survive config reloads, and `/` opens Go To search on layouts where it needs Shift. (#4960, #4963, #4983, #5090, thanks @minatoaquaMK2) - Pasting from WezTerm on Windows into remote panes no longer types encoded key data. Copying no longer adds duplicate clipboard history entries, and closing a pane no longer hangs on a pending Enter. (#4725, #4730, #4709, #4872, thanks @JJLiebig) - Windows shells started through Scoop or 32-bit wrappers keep their working directory in new tabs and after restarts. (#4472, #4749, #5062, thanks @JJLiebig) - Windows ARM64 machines can be used as remote hosts through the x64 build, on a best-effort basis. (#4752) - Codex reports finished turns through its integration when started with `--no-daemon`, its startup update screen shows as blocked, and Codex and Pi status is recognized in more cases. (#4507, #4811, #4756, #5174, thanks @JJLiebig) - Antigravity permission, question and workspace-trust dialogs show as blocked, and its thinking spinner shows as working. (#3871, #3419, #3530, #4959) - Hermes started through its Python installer is recognized, and installing or removing its integration keeps your YAML plugin list intact. (#4910, #4909, #4911, #4984, thanks @minatoaquaMK2) - On macOS, `pane run` and `agent start` refuse commands too long for a shell that is still starting, instead of silently cutting them off. (#4950, #4988, thanks @minatoaquaMK2)  ### Removed - The experimental `HERDR_WINDOWS_INPUT_BACKEND=crossterm` setting is gone. Windows always uses its native console input. (#5017) 
```

新增漏洞复测功能，可从漏洞列表一键创建独立复测对话，继承原始对话历史与模型上下文，支持多轮验证修复结果。复测提示词自动带入已有验证方式，并支持漏洞与会话 ID 占位符替换；验证完成后可更新漏洞状态，追加保存复测证据，便于持续跟踪修复进展。  ---  Added vulnerability retesting with a dedicated conversation branch created directly from the vulnerability list. Each branch inherits the original conversation history and model context, supporting multiple rounds of fix verification. Retest prompts include existing verification guidance and resolve vulnerability and conversation ID placeholders. Findings can then be updated with their verification status and supporting evidence to track remediation progress.
