### 2026-10-09T05:40:10Z [**Gloridust/WechatOnCloud**](https://github.com/Gloridust/WechatOnCloud)

```
## ⬆️ 升级方式（重要）  1. 面板「检查更新」把面板升到 1.5.2（在 `.env` 里固定了 `WOC_VERSION` 的，改成 `1.5.2` 后执行 `docker compose pull && docker compose up -d`）； 2. **再点「一键升级全部实例」**：QQ 下载的修复在实例镜像里，**只更新面板不会生效**。  升级实例会保留全部数据（登录状态、聊天记录、设备标识）。之前 QQ 下载失败的实例，升级后回到实例卡片重新点「下载安装」即可。  ---  ## 🐛 修复  ### QQ 下载不了，提示 HTTP 403（#153） 1.5.1 里所有 QQ 实例都下载不了，国内网络也一样。原因是腾讯给 QQ 的下载地址加了签名校验，不带签名的请求一律 403，跟网络在哪无关。1.5.1 的提示说「只对中国大陆网络开放」是判断错了，关代理、换直连都不会有用。  现在安装时会先向腾讯官网换取带签名的下载链接再下载；中途断了会重新签名、从断点续传。实测境外网络也能正常下载、安装，并启动到扫码登录界面。感谢 @SmartElec 定位并提交修复（#156）。  ### 无感输入：拼音上屏后紧接着按空格，空格跑到文字前面（#155） 打「你好」后连按空格，远端收到的是「 你好」。现在中文还在送出时按下的空格，会排在它后面按顺序送出；没有中文在送出时，空格照旧直接发送，不加延迟。底部输入条（转发模式）原本就没有这个问题。  ---  ## 🔧 改进  - **诊断包多了「桌面服务」一段**：每个实例附上桌面入口（nginx）和远程桌面（KasmVNC）有没有在监听，以及 nginx 的错误日志。遇到「正在连接桌面」「桌面长时间未就绪」时，导出的诊断包能直接看出卡在哪里（#107、#114）。 - 自己构建镜像的 `scripts/build-local.sh`：镜像名称改成跟 `docker-compose.yml` 用同一个 `WOC_IMAGE_PREFIX`。之前构建完直接 `docker compose up -d` 会去拉官方镜像，而不是用刚构建好的。  ---  ## ↩️ 回滚  出现问题时可回到 1.5.1：在 `.env` 里设 `WOC_VERSION=1.5.1`，执行 `docker compose up -d`，再在面板里「一键升级全部实例」（实例切回 1.5.1 镜像，数据保留）。已经装好的 QQ 在 1.5.1 下照常运行，只是在 1.5.1 下重新下载 / 更新 QQ 会再遇到 403。  镜像：`ghcr.io/gloridust/{woc-panel,wechat-on-cloud}:1.5.2`、`gloridust/{woc-panel,wechat-on-cloud}:1.5.2`（amd64 + arm64）。 
```

### 2026-10-13T18:46:22Z [**AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm**](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm)

```
[中文](#中文) | [English](#english)  ### 中文  OxideTerm 2.2.3 新增动态背景、可叠加的视觉效果和按需安装的背景素材包，允许为系统浅色与深色模式分别配置完整外观，并重新整理首次使用引导。同时明确云同步的下载、合并与上传方向，修复 SSH 会话重连、Linux 粘贴编辑、macOS 远程桌面文件剪贴板，以及更新与便携版数据保留问题。  #### 🎨 完整亮暗外观与背景编辑  - “跟随系统外观”支持分别配置浅色和深色两套外观，每套包含应用主题、终端主题与背景。切换系统亮暗模式时，界面、现有终端和背景一起更新，无须重新连接会话。 - 背景设置将图片或视频素材、叠加特效和背景交互分区展示；可以单独使用程序生成的效果，也可以把效果叠加在图片、动图或视频上。 - 新增九点对齐，结合现有适配方式决定裁切后保留的位置；素材不透明度、模糊、阅读遮罩、作用范围和启用页面可分别调整。 - 主题预览与编辑预览同时呈现背景、透明度和裁切效果。主题编辑使用独立草稿，取消后恢复原来的外观。 - 素材卡片左上角显示文件格式，右上角保留删除按钮，并标明当前使用的素材。修复部分视频和导入图片无法显示缩略图的问题。 - 按素材和效果的实际能力显示参数，静态内容不再出现无效的播放速度等控制。修复新增滑杆点击、拖动时偏离鼠标的位置，以及切换选项后设置页滚动位置跳动的问题。  #### ✨ 程序生成效果与背景交互  - 新增轻纹理、雾光、光潮、光潮与粒子组合、流星、景深粒子和水面折光；各效果提供适用的强度、颜色、大小、速度、方向、粗糙度、光泽或密度控制，默认颜色可取自当前主题。 - 特效强度与素材不透明度分别控制，统一有无素材时的强度表现，改善特效叠加在图片、视频和动图上过淡的问题。 - 新增“输入时暂停动效”，连续输入时暂停动态素材和效果，停止输入约一秒后恢复。此选项由用户开启。 - 新增背景视差，静态图片和效果层可随鼠标轻微移动；景深粒子的不同层使用不同位移幅度，终端文字保持固定。 - 新增静态图片的缓慢运镜，支持平移、推近和拉远，可调整幅度与速度，并在往返运动时保留画面边缘覆盖。 - 新增全天候光照，按本地时间渐变背景光线与色彩，并可预览清晨、午后、傍晚和夜间。应用与终端主题仍按各自的亮暗配置切换。 - 背景切换增加平滑过渡；窗口不活跃、不可见或最小化时暂停播放，并遵循现有减少动态效果设置。  #### 🎬 原生视频播放与按需素材包  - 视频背景接入 macOS 系统媒体解码、Windows Media Foundation 和 Linux GStreamer；支持原生视频缓冲或纹理进入对应图形后端，在能力允许时减少 CPU 拷贝与重复上传。 - 保留软件解码和像素上传路径；自动模式按持续处理负载调整播放尺寸与帧率，用户手动设置的上限保持不变。硬件加速是否可用取决于设备、驱动和视频格式。 - 背景与预览复用同一媒体处理结果，按视频时间调度显示，限制提前解码和缓冲占用；将支持路径上的缩放、裁切与模糊交给图形后端处理。 - 修复 Windows 视频缓冲未提供行跨度时导致缩略图和背景不显示的问题；修复 4K 软件缩放后不透明视频的颜色与透明度处理。 - Linux 发行包补齐媒体解码组件及插件扫描器定位，使打包后的程序能够发现自带的解码支持。 - 新增插件市场“背景素材”分类与素材库“获取更多背景”入口。视频素材独立于主程序分发，可按需安装，主程序保留现有静态背景。 - 背景包安装后将声明的素材导入当前配置的素材库；便携版使用自己的数据目录。安装不改变当前主题和背景，卸载包后已导入素材继续保留，可在素材库单独删除。 - 已发布的 OxideTerm Backgrounds 1.0.0 提供“晨雾”和“夜流”两段原创 3840 × 2160、24 帧/秒、16 秒循环视频，适用于浅色和深色外观；需使用本次或更高的主程序版本安装。  #### 🧭 首次使用引导与界面体验  - 首次使用引导整理为五页：欢迎与须知、外观、连接与操作、AI 与命令行、开始使用。宽窗口采用分栏，窄窗口自动调整布局，内容区随窗口尺寸变化。 - 首页使用独立应用标记与标题，保留语言选择和必要说明；主题预览集中在外观页，最后一页聚焦打开终端、新建连接和导入连接。 - 步骤导航和页数居中，复用现有切换与入场动画；按钮颜色跟随当前主题，提示与说明重新整理为更直接的引导文案。 - 新用户只显示欢迎引导，不再同时显示旧版迁移介绍；完成引导前先保存状态，避免重新启动后重复出现。保存失败时保留引导并给出提示。（Issue [#666](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/issues/666)） - 半透明面板与侧边区域新增随主题和透明度调整的柔和边缘层次，改善背景下分割线突兀的问题；背景和媒体绘制保留圆角裁切。 - 新建连接表单改用已安装插件的状态判断，避免绘制时反复探测外部程序；SSH 代理发现也减少阻塞探测，改善 Windows 打开表单和切换远程桌面页面的卡顿。 - 调整 Windows 背景与界面绘制路径，减少重复图形工作；修复依赖真实时间的弹层关闭状态，保持界面动画时钟一致。  #### ☁️ 云同步方向与首次下载  - 新增只读“查看云端”入口，允许先检查云端配置，再决定是否应用。 - 同步预览明确提供“使用云端配置”“合并本机与云端”“用本机更新云端”三种方向，并使用对应的说明和确认按钮。 - 新设备首次发现已有云端配置时，默认预览“使用云端配置”，确认后下载应用并建立同步基线，不上传本机默认设置；首次自动同步同样需要明确确认。（Discussion [#663](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/discussions/663)） - 预览中的本机更改保留在计划内，取消不会提前推进已保存的同步基线。合并时重新读取本机当前设置，纳入预览期间发生的本机更改；各方向均保留未选同步范围，云端自身存在冲突时，下载前需完成选择。 - 下载完成后记录正确的云端状态与下载历史，保留现有恢复机制和同步密码修改流程。  #### 🔌 终端、SSH、SFTP 与远程桌面修复  - SSH 远端 Shell 退出后，可通过原窗格的重连按钮或 Enter 恢复会话，保留窗格身份和滚动历史；仍在使用的 SFTP、端口转发和其他消费者继续持有共享连接。（Issue [#669](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/issues/669)） - 远程 Shell 集成失败时显示具体原因，包括 SFTP 不可用、不支持的 Shell、读写权限和安装未完成，并明确终端本身是否仍可使用，避免把可选集成问题误认为 SSH 连接失败。（Issue [#664](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/issues/664)） - 修复 Linux 多行粘贴编辑窗口只能退格、删除却无法输入字符的问题；文字输入进入现有编辑器，编辑期间不会被转发给后台终端，确认粘贴后才发送。（Issue [#677](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/issues/677)） - 修复 SFTP 文件拖放释放鼠标时先清理拖动状态的问题，使跨窗格拖放正确进入上传、下载或冲突处理流程。 - 修复 macOS RDP 文件剪贴板同步，连接建立或切回窗口时同步本地复制的文件，使远端菜单能直接识别可粘贴文件，并避免把远端文件重复回传。（Issue [#670](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/issues/670)） - 修复 Linux 客户端标题栏关闭窗口时的工作区重入问题。  #### 📦 更新与便携版  - 更新完成后先释放当前工作区更新，再执行保存历史和退出流程，修复 macOS 旧进程在更新退出时出现崩溃提示的问题。 - Windows 应用内更新统一由主程序启动更新辅助程序，等待旧进程结束后替换并启动新版，避免安装器重复启动辅助程序影响自动重启。 - 启用便携存储时，更新包仍匹配当前运行形式：macOS 应用包、Linux AppImage 和解压式便携版分别使用对应安装包。 - macOS 替换应用包时保留包内的便携标记、配置和数据目录；替换或数据恢复失败时回滚，避免更新后丢失便携数据。  #### 📌 升级说明  - 背景动效、输入时暂停、视差、运镜和全天候光照均可自行选择；需要保持静态外观时可关闭这些选项。播放性能和可用加速路径随硬件、驱动及媒体格式变化，可在自动模式之外手动设置分辨率和帧率。 - 视频素材包从插件市场按需安装，不随主程序携带。背景包导入的视频会占用当前配置的数据目录空间；卸载插件不会自动删除这些已导入的素材。 - 更新退出、安装和重启修复由新版客户端执行，从旧版首次升级时，旧客户端仍使用原有更新逻辑。 - 本版更新日志范围：[v2.2.2…v2.2.3](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/compare/v2.2.2...v2.2.3)。  ### English  OxideTerm 2.2.3 adds animated backgrounds, composable visual effects, and on-demand background packs, with complete appearance profiles for the system's light and dark modes and a redesigned first-run guide. It also makes cloud download, merge, and upload directions explicit and fixes SSH session reconnection, Linux paste editing, macOS remote desktop file clipboard synchronization, and update handling for installed and portable builds.  #### 🎨 Complete light and dark appearances and background editing  - Follow System Appearance now supports separate light and dark profiles, each containing an application theme, terminal theme, and background. System appearance changes update the interface, existing terminals, and background together without reconnecting sessions. - Background settings separate image or video media, overlay effects, and interactions. Generated effects can stand alone or be layered over images, animations, and videos. - Added nine-point alignment to control which part of a background remains visible after cropping. Media opacity, blur, a readability overlay, background scope, and enabled pages can be configured separately. - Theme and editor previews show the background, transparency, and crop together. Theme editing uses a separate draft; cancellation restores the previous appearance. - Media cards show their file format at the top left, retain deletion at the top right, and identify the active item. Fixed missing thumbnails for some videos and imported images. - Controls now reflect the selected media and effect, hiding irrelevant playback-speed controls for static content. Fixed pointer offsets in new sliders and settings scroll jumps after changing options.  #### ✨ Generated effects and background interactions  - Added subtle texture, fog glow, light tides, combined tides and particles, meteors, depth particles, and water caustics. Effects expose applicable strength, color, size, speed, direction, roughness, sheen, or density controls, with default colors derived from the current theme. - Effect strength and media opacity are independent. Strength behaves consistently with or without media, improving overlays that were too faint on images, videos, and animations. - Added an optional Pause Effects While Typing setting. Animated media and effects pause during continuous input and resume approximately one second after typing stops. - Added background parallax for static images and effect layers. Depth-particle layers move by different amounts while terminal text stays fixed. - Added slow camera motion for static images, with pan, zoom-in, and zoom-out options, adjustable amount and speed, and edge coverage throughout the repeating motion. - Added all-day lighting that gradually changes background light and color with local time, with morning, afternoon, evening, and night previews. Application and terminal themes continue to follow their configured light and dark profiles. - Background changes now transition smoothly. Playback pauses when the window is inactive, hidden, or minimized and follows the existing reduced-motion settings.  #### 🎬 Native video playback and on-demand background packs  - Video backgrounds use macOS system media decoding, Windows Media Foundation, and Linux GStreamer. Native video buffers or textures can enter the corresponding graphics backend to reduce CPU copies and repeated uploads where supported. - Software decoding and pixel-upload paths remain available. Automatic mode adjusts playback dimensions and frame rate under sustained processing load while preserving manually configured limits. Hardware acceleration depends on the device, driver, and video format. - Backgrounds and previews share media processing results, schedule presentation by video time, and bound decode-ahead work and buffering. Supported paths perform scaling, cropping, and blur in the graphics backend. - Fixed missing Windows video thumbnails and backgrounds when decoded buffers omit their row stride. Fixed color and opacity handling for opaque videos resized at 4K through the software path. - Linux packages now include media decoding components and locate the bundled plugin scanner so packaged applications can discover their decoder support. - Added a Background Media marketplace category and a Get More Backgrounds entry in the media library. Videos are distributed independently as optional packs; the application retains its existing static backgrounds. - Installing a pack imports its declared media into the current configuration's library, including the portable data directory. Installation leaves the current theme and background unchanged. Imported media remains after uninstalling the pack and can be deleted separately from the library. - The published OxideTerm Backgrounds 1.0.0 pack provides two original 3840 × 2160, 24 fps, 16-second loops, Dawn Mist and Night Flow, for light and dark appearances. Installation requires this host release or a later version.  #### 🧭 First-run guide and interface experience  - Reorganized onboarding into five pages: Welcome and Notices, Appearance, Connections and Operations, AI and Command Line, and Getting Started. Wide windows use columns; narrow windows adapt the layout, and the content area follows the window size. - The first page uses a separate application mark and title, retaining language selection and essential information. Theme preview is concentrated on Appearance, and the final page focuses on opening a terminal, creating a connection, and importing connections. - Centered step navigation and page numbering, reused existing entry and switching animations, made button colors follow the theme, and rewrote guidance more directly. - New users see onboarding without the old-version migration introduction. Completion is saved before closing to avoid showing the guide again after restart; a failed save keeps the guide open with a message. (Issue [#666](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/issues/666)) - Translucent panels and sidebar regions now have softer edge definition that follows the theme and opacity, improving abrupt separators over backgrounds. Background and media rendering retain rounded clipping. - Connection forms use installed-plugin state instead of repeatedly probing external programs during rendering. SSH agent discovery also avoids blocking probes, improving responsiveness when opening forms and switching remote desktop pages on Windows. - Updated Windows background and interface rendering to reduce repeated graphics work. Fixed overlay dismissal states that depended on wall-clock time so they use the interface animation clock consistently.  #### ☁️ Cloud sync direction and first-device downloads  - Added a read-only View Cloud Configuration entry to inspect remote configuration before applying it. - Sync previews explicitly offer Use Cloud Configuration, Merge Local and Cloud, and Update Cloud from This Device, with matching explanations and confirmation buttons. - A new device discovering existing cloud configuration defaults to previewing Use Cloud Configuration. Confirmation downloads and applies it, establishes a sync baseline, and does not upload local defaults. Initial automatic sync also requires explicit confirmation. (Discussion [#663](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/discussions/663)) - Local preview edits remain in the plan; cancellation does not advance the saved sync baseline. Merging re-reads current local configuration to include edits made during the preview. All directions preserve excluded scope, and downloads require choices for conflicts already present in the cloud. - Download completion records the correct cloud state and download history, retaining existing recovery and sync-password-change flows.  #### 🔌 Terminal, SSH, SFTP, and remote desktop fixes  - After a remote SSH shell exits, its reconnect button or Enter restores the session in the original pane, preserving pane identity and scrollback. SFTP, forwarding, and other consumers retain their ownership of the shared connection. (Issue [#669](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/issues/669)) - Remote shell integration failures now explain the cause, including unavailable SFTP, unsupported shells, read/write permissions, and incomplete installation, and clarify whether the SSH terminal remains usable. (Issue [#664](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/issues/664)) - Fixed the Linux multiline paste editor accepting Backspace and Delete but not typed characters. Text reaches the existing editor, editing keys are not forwarded to the background terminal, and content is sent only after confirming the paste. (Issue [#677](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/issues/677)) - Fixed SFTP mouse release clearing drag state before completing a drop, restoring upload, download, and conflict handling across panes. - Fixed macOS RDP file clipboard synchronization. Connecting or returning to the window advertises locally copied files so the remote menu can recognize available files without a preceding paste shortcut, while avoiding echoing remote-origin files. (Issue [#670](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/issues/670)) - Fixed workspace reentry when closing a Linux window through its client-side titlebar.  #### 📦 Updates and portable builds  - Update completion releases the current workspace update before saving history and quitting, fixing the macOS old process reporting a crash during update shutdown. - Windows in-app updates now launch the helper from the application, wait for the old process to exit, replace files, and start the new version, avoiding duplicate helper launches from the installer that interfered with automatic restart. - Portable storage preserves the running package's update format: macOS application bundles, Linux AppImages, and extracted portable builds each select their corresponding package. - macOS application replacement preserves bundle-local portable markers, configuration, and data directories, with rollback if replacement or data restoration fails.  #### 📌 Upgrade notes  - Background effects, typing pauses, parallax, camera motion, and all-day lighting are optional. Disable them for a static appearance. Playback performance and acceleration depend on hardware, drivers, and media formats; resolution and frame rate can be set manually instead of using automatic mode. - Video packs are installed on demand from the marketplace and are not bundled with the application. Imported videos occupy space in the current configuration's data directory; uninstalling a plugin does not automatically delete imported media. - Update shutdown, installation, and restart fixes are performed by the new client. The first upgrade from an older version still uses that older client's update logic. - Full release range: [v2.2.2…v2.2.3](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/compare/v2.2.2...v2.2.3).  ## 📥 Download for your system  | Operating system | x64 | ARM64 | |---|---|---| | **Windows** | [Setup (.exe)](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.2.3/OxideTerm_2.2.3_windows_x64-setup.exe) | [Setup (.exe)](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.2.3/OxideTerm_2.2.3_windows_arm64-setup.exe) | | **macOS** | [DMG (Intel)](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.2.3/OxideTerm_2.2.3_macos_x64.dmg) | [DMG (Apple Silicon)](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.2.3/OxideTerm_2.2.3_macos_arm64.dmg) | | **Linux** | [AppImage](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.2.3/OxideTerm_2.2.3_linux_x64.AppImage) · [DEB](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.2.3/OxideTerm_2.2.3_linux_x64.deb) · [RPM](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.2.3/OxideTerm_2.2.3_linux_x64.rpm) | [AppImage](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.2.3/OxideTerm_2.2.3_linux_arm64.AppImage) · [DEB](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.2.3/OxideTerm_2.2.3_linux_arm64.deb) · [RPM](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.2.3/OxideTerm_2.2.3_linux_arm64.rpm) |  Portable archives, signatures, and `sha256sums.txt` remain available in the release assets below.  ## 插件市场兼容说明  插件市场 v1 已冻结，原地址、索引内容和对应安装包持续保留。旧客户端仍可使用冻结目录中的插件，但不会看到后续的新插件和更新；请升级到支持 v2 目录的主程序版本。后续插件、新版本和版本历史仅发布到 v2。  ## Plugin marketplace compatibility  The v1 plugin marketplace is frozen. Its original URL, exact contents, and referenced packages remain available. Existing clients can keep using the frozen catalog but will not see subsequent plugins or updates; upgrade to a host version supporting v2 to receive them. All subsequent plugins, releases, and version histories are published only to v2.  <details> <summary>📌 Installation Tips / 安装提示</summary>  ### macOS  Downloaded `.dmg` files may be quarantined by Gatekeeper. Run in Terminal:  bash xattr -cr ~/Downloads/OxideTerm_*.dmg # or after install / 或安装后 xattr -cr /Applications/OxideTerm.app   ### Windows  If SmartScreen warns, click **More info** -> **Run anyway**.  若 SmartScreen 弹出警告，点击 **更多信息** -> **仍要运行**。  ### Linux  bash # AppImage chmod +x OxideTerm_*_linux_*.AppImage && ./OxideTerm_*_linux_*.AppImage  # Debian/Ubuntu sudo dpkg -i OxideTerm_*_linux_*.deb && sudo apt-get install -f  # Fedora/RHEL-compatible systems sudo dnf install ./OxideTerm_*_linux_*.rpm   </details>  ## 🔗 Links  - Documentation: https://oxideterm.app - GitHub Issues: https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/issues - Changelog: https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/blob/main/.github/release-notes/stable-changelog.md

## [1.0.0](https://github.com/rtk-ai/rtk/compare/v0.52.0...v1.0.0) (2026-10-13)   ### ⚠ BREAKING CHANGES  * **cli:** `rtk proxy <cmd>` is now `rtk bypass <cmd>` (the old name stays as an alias), `--ultra-compact` is removed, `rtk gain` excludes oversized outputs from its headline rate, and the default binary's licence changes (an Apache-2.0 edition, rtk-oss, ships alongside). ## What's Changed * (IMPORTANT) `/loop` is being deprecated; it will still be avaialbe until the next major release, but it won't be found in the set of default compile-time features * (IMPORTANT) The `zerostack-lite-[target]` family of binary builds now ships with `--default-features` instead of `--no-default-features`, while the standard build still ships with all features enabled * feat(ui): Ctrl+Left, Ctrl+Right, paste in input and selection in input; by @0f3b79350c99d  * feat(status-signals): report permission waits (protocol v1.1) by @xavierforge in https://github.com/gi-dellav/zerostack/pull/282 * feat(cli): added `--disable-lazygit` (Ctrl+L shortcut), used by `multistack` * add darcs commands to permissions by @toastal in https://github.com/gi-dellav/zerostack/pull/299 * feat(provider): add opencode-zen and opencode-go by @shramee in https://github.com/gi-dellav/zerostack/pull/294 * prompt: optimized write-text.md for better prose (higher average word-per-phrase count) * prompt: optimized code.md for reduced amount of `find`/`grep` calls (works best on recent models) * tweak: tweaked default values for compaction and for tool call (the compaction kicks off less often; grep/find now provide more outputs) * docs: add Opper custom provider recipe by @Felixkw12 in https://github.com/gi-dellav/zerostack/pull/306 * fix(ui): make bash allow-always patterns match paths by @NotAFlightRisk in https://github.com/gi-dellav/zerostack/pull/281 * fix(ci): clear engine clippy lints and the advisor panic under --all-features by @xavierforge in https://github.com/gi-dellav/zerostack/pull/286 * fix(agent): group parallel tool calls and results in history by @shurizzle in https://github.com/gi-dellav/zerostack/pull/297 * build: bump the cargo group across 1 directory with 4 updates by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/gi-dellav/zerostack/pull/309 * internal: rewrote code around `Engine` struct, allowing for easier edits * internal: updated deps to latest version  * internal: moved `logging` logic to compile-time feature (enabled by default) * chore: refresh model catalog from models.dev by @xavierforge in https://github.com/gi-dellav/zerostack/pull/308  ## New Contributors * @NotAFlightRisk made their first contribution in https://github.com/gi-dellav/zerostack/pull/281 * @Felixkw12 made their first contribution in https://github.com/gi-dellav/zerostack/pull/306 * @shurizzle made their first contribution in https://github.com/gi-dellav/zerostack/pull/297 * @shramee made their first contribution in https://github.com/gi-dellav/zerostack/pull/294  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/gi-dellav/zerostack/compare/v1.8.4...v1.8.5

### 2026-10-14T02:12:10Z [**t8y2/dbx**](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx)

```
### 赞助信息  - 「重生为职场龙傲天之我有半价 buff」：Jalapeno Cloud 半价提供 GLM 5.3 与 DS V4.1 Flash，可叠加多充多送 20% 加赠，[活动入口](https://www.jalapeno-cloud.ai/DBX)  ### 新功能  - 网格导出支持按列格式化输出，导出的 CSV、JSON、XLSX 与表格显示格式一致 (contributed by @lxk955) (PR [#11945](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/11945)) - 侧边栏新增快捷键（默认 F4）直接打开当前选中对象的表结构编辑器 (contributed by @lxk955) (PR [#12037](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12037)) - 表结构编辑器中按 F5 即可刷新当前表结构 (contributed by @kkwangxx) (PR [#11858](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/11858)) - 标签页支持用 Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+T 或右键菜单重新打开最近关闭的标签页 (contributed by @lxk955) (PR [#11651](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/11651)) - 数据网格视图选项新增「全局冻结首列」，横向滚动时保持首列固定 (contributed by @eryajf) (PR [#11633](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/11633)) - 递增 ID 生成支持补零位数、自定义前缀和后缀，并实时预览生成效果 (contributed by @lxk955) (PR [#11937](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/11937)) - JSON 值编辑器支持节点折叠，折叠箭头改用统一的 SVG 图标，避免在部分字体下显示为空框 (contributed by @lxk955) (PR [#11938](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/11938)) - 快捷键搜索新增「录制按键」模式，按下组合键即可筛选出绑定该键的操作 (contributed by @Abeautifulsnow) (PR [#11949](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/11949)) - OceanBase Oracle 模式的 SQL 补全支持建议序列名以及 NEXTVAL/CURRVAL 伪列 (contributed by @lxk955) (PR [#12073](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12073)) - SAP HANA 支持表重命名和表结构编辑，包括新增、删除、重命名列和修改列类型、默认值、可空性 (contributed by @zipg) (PR [#12036](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12036)) - Redis 值查看器搜索统一高亮和导航，并新增大小写、正则开关以及单项和全部替换 (contributed by @eryajf) (PR [#12039](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12039)) - MQTT 消息右键支持复制 Payload、复制 Topic 和删除本地消息记录 (contributed by @ma123456mai) (PR [#11988](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/11988)) - MQTT 新增/编辑订阅弹框支持按标题栏拖动 (contributed by @ma123456mai) (PR [#11942](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/11942)) - 设置中显示 WebDAV 最近一次成功同步的时间，按同步目标分别记录 (contributed by @ma123456mai) (PR [#12065](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12065)) - AI 设置新增配置导入入口，可通过 CC-SWITCH 插件导入已有 AI 配置 (contributed by @ma123456mai) (PR [#9365](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/9365)) - 生产环境徽章支持配置显示范围（连接、库/模式或表/对象），侧边栏滚动固定上下文的交接更准确 (contributed by @zipg) (PR [#11995](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/11995))  ### 改进  - MongoDB 查询的总数统计与查询本身并发执行，慢查询下总耗时由两者之和降为两者较大值 (contributed by @thailoc-dev) (PR [#11978](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/11978)) - 横版标签栏只有选中的标签保留连接色底色，开启连接分组后更容易定位当前标签 (contributed by @zipg) (PR [#12060](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12060))  ### 修复  - macOS 应用图标不再跟随应用主题切换，Dock 和 Finder 中保持稳定显示 - 最大化窗口重启后恢复到原所在的显示器，不再错位到其他屏幕 - 数据对比结果被截断时不再允许选中执行同步，避免生成不完整的同步 SQL - 便携版数据迁移密钥可跨设备保留，换电脑后不再误报数据安全升级 - Oracle 系语句切分不再在 WITH FUNCTION 内联声明的内部分号处截断，达梦执行此类语句不再报语法错误 (contributed by @yiqiui) (PR [#12080](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12080)) - Oracle、OceanBase Oracle、达梦、YashanDB、Oscar 等 Oracle 系方言不再把字符串中的反斜杠当作引号转义，含反斜杠结尾字符串的批次不再被错误拆分 (contributed by @lxk955) (PR [#11951](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/11951)) - 结果网格选区的 SUM 和 AVG 对 BIGINT、DECIMAL 按精确十进制计算，不再出现末位进位错误 (contributed by @jeff377) (PR [#12078](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12078)) - CSV/JSON/XLSX 导出保留 CLOB 列的格式化结果，不再被后端完整原值覆盖 (contributed by @efeire) (PR [#12069](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12069)) - Oracle 和 OceanBase Oracle 的 SQL 导出改用原始值构造，BLOB 数据回放不再报 ORA-01465 (contributed by @efeire) (PR [#12069](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12069)) - Oracle 和 OceanBase Oracle 表结构编辑不再丢失 FLOAT 二进制精度、NUMBER 精度和负 scale 等数值参数 (contributed by @efeire) (PR [#12042](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12042)) - OceanBase Oracle 常规表结构克隆保留主键、索引、默认值、非空和注释，不再退化为 CTAS 丢结构 (contributed by @efeire) (PR [#12042](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12042)) - 数据传输到 Oracle 目标复制显式 id 后自动把 identity 序列重置到最大值之后，避免后续自增插入与已复制数据冲突 (contributed by @Tong-bit-art) (PR [#12001](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12001)) - Oracle 连接 URL（含密码）复制时密码中的 ^ 等字符保持可读，粘贴到其他软件后凭据一致 (contributed by @zipg) (PR [#12020](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12020)) - SAP HANA 执行含过程体的 ALTER/CREATE PROCEDURE、FUNCTION、TRIGGER 不再被内部分号截断报 257 语法错误 (contributed by @zipg) (PR [#11997](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/11997)) - Trino 查看表 DDL 改用 SHOW CREATE TABLE，不再丢失 WITH 子句中的物理布局定义 (contributed by @hongshuo-wang) (PR [#11991](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/11991)) - SUNDB 生成的编辑和查询 SQL 不再给简单标识符加双引号，编辑数据保存不再报表或视图不存在 (contributed by @hongshuo-wang) (PR [#12002](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12002)) - GaussDB M 模式下统计总行数不再因派生表别名被双引号包裹而报语法错误 (contributed by @AndrewDi) (PR [#12040](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12040)) - 达梦账号缺少 DBMS_OUTPUT 权限时自动降级跳过初始化，不再阻断用户自己的 CALL/BEGIN/EXEC 语句 (contributed by @hongshuo-wang) (PR [#11959](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/11959)) - 用 Neo4j 连接类型可直连单实例 Memgraph，且不再向其发送不支持的默认库名 (contributed by @hongshuo-wang) (PR [#11975](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/11975)) - Excel/CSV 导入数字类型列时接受带千分位的数值如 123,456，文本列保持原样 (contributed by @hongshuo-wang) (PR [#11999](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/11999)) - MongoDB 集合网格编辑 Int32 和 Double 字段不再被保存成 Int64，类型保持不变 (contributed by @thailoc-dev) (PR [#11950](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/11950)) - SSH 隧道密钥认证未填路径时自动按 OpenSSH 默认顺序回落到本机默认私钥 (contributed by @hongshuo-wang) (PR [#11965](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/11965)) - Oracle 系方言中 @dblink 链接不再被误识别为绑定参数或被同名变量替换 (contributed by @efeire) (PR [#11780](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/11780)) - 手工事务提交不再重复请求同一事务，批量执行补齐超时传递、取消跟踪和回滚后的连接回收 (contributed by @efeire) (PR [#11780](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/11780)) - 前一条 SQL 缺少分号时自定义代码片段也能正常弹出补全菜单 (contributed by @lxk955) (PR [#11936](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/11936)) - 按 F7 不再触发浏览器自带的光标浏览确认弹窗，并在编辑器设置中新增光标浏览开关 (contributed by @lxk955) (PR [#11934](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/11934)) - 列查找面板打开期间保留搜索词和匹配列表，连续跳转列不再重复输入 (contributed by @eryajf) (PR [#12035](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12035)) - 右键菜单的复制和导出子菜单项继承父项图标，不再出现空白图标槽 (contributed by @zipg) (PR [#11867](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/11867)) - 查询区域高度较小时生产水印自适应切换为单行或单个显示，不再文字重叠 (contributed by @zipg) (PR [#12047](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12047)) - MQTT 消息列表改为独立卡片展示并增加间距，时间戳统一为本地 YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss 格式 (contributed by @ma123456mai) (PR [#11984](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/11984))  --- <!-- dbx-cnb-mirror --> > 国内下载：如果 GitHub 下载较慢，可从 [CNB 镜像](https://cnb.cool/dbxio.com/dbx/-/releases/tag/v0.6.43) 下载桌面端安装包，Docker 镜像从 `docker.cnb.cool/dbxio.com/dbx:v0.6.43` 拉取。

### 2026-10-13T18:34:04Z [**JuliusBrussee/caveman**](https://github.com/JuliusBrussee/caveman)

```
Caveman 4.0.1 fixes what turned up in the first hours of 4.0.0: a Windows uninstall bug, a sync that could never catch up, and problems on OpenCode 2 and Pi.  ### Windows  - `caveman off --all` left the Caveman Blocks hook in Claude Code's settings, and every `caveman setup` added another copy. Blocks v0.1.0-rc.5 installs its binary as `.exe`, recognises the entries rc.4 wrote, and replaces them on the next `caveman setup`. - An npm `.cmd` shim that forwards to a native `.exe` launches again. - Hook child processes no longer flash a console window.  ### Sync  - `caveman sync` uploads a large backlog of local spans in batches. Before, a store over the 16 MiB import cap failed with "Request body exceeds configured limit" on every retry. - The `ExperimentalWarning: SQLite` line is gone. - Known issue: the practice-findings part of `caveman sync` still reports "Request rejected". That is a Caveman Cloud bug and needs no CLI update once Cloud is fixed.  ### OpenCode  - On OpenCode 2, a subagent spawn reset the mode for every session, undoing "stop caveman" and `/ultracave`. Subagent sessions no longer touch the mode. - The "provider is not routed" warning reads where a provider actually sends requests.  ### Pi  - `caveman wrap pi` after `caveman enable pi` loaded the extension twice and Pi exited 1. - Routing a model no longer resets the thinking level (`--thinking high` stayed only until session start). - Works with Pi 1.1+ custom-message turns. - `@caveman-ai/pi` 0.3.1: the npm package now reads Caveman 4's config and port.  ### Claude Code and Codex  - The prompt hook gives up after 2 seconds when the host sends no complete payload, like the other hooks. - Blocks honors `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` and `CODEX_HOME`; it used to write the hook into `~/.claude` regardless. - `caveman status` no longer says "account refresh offline" after signing in to a different account.  ### Smaller fixes  - Proxy: one key sent in both auth headers keeps its bearer; a Termux-built proxy is recognised as Caveman's own. - Memory: longer retry on SQLite contention. - `/caveman-compress` asks for a file path instead of improvising on pasted text, and says that the file goes to your model provider. - Tested agent pins moved to current Claude Code, Codex, OpenCode, Gemini, Kilo and Qwen releases.  ### Versions  - `@caveman-ai/cli` 4.0.0 → 4.0.1, binaries `bin-v4.0.0` → `bin-v4.0.1`, `@caveman-ai/pi` 0.3.0 → 0.3.1, Blocks v0.1.0-rc.4 → v0.1.0-rc.5.  ### Companion releases  - [Signed binaries, module index and container: `bin-v4.0.1`](https://github.com/JuliusBrussee/caveman/releases/tag/bin-v4.0.1) - [npm CLI: `@caveman-ai/cli@4.0.1`](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@caveman-ai/cli/v/4.0.1) - [npm Pi extension: `@caveman-ai/pi@0.3.1`](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@caveman-ai/pi/v/0.3.1) 
```

### 2026-10-09T03:29:08Z [**foru17/neko-master**](https://github.com/foru17/neko-master)

```
This release adds a few small features that users have asked for repeatedly. The agent ships alongside as [agent-v1.4.8](https://github.com/foru17/neko-master/releases/tag/agent-v1.4.8).  ## Added - **Live speed** (#93): the Total Download and Total Upload cards now show the current speed. The trend chart has a new Traffic / Speed toggle; speed mode plots only completed time buckets, so the right edge no longer dips while the newest bucket is still filling. - **Export domains** (#54): domain lists in the rule, proxy and device views, and in the IP expand panel, can be exported as a txt file with one domain per line, ready for your own rule-set. - **`LOG_LEVEL` environment variable** (#80): controls how much the collector logs. Accepts `error`, `warn`, `info` (default) or `debug`. - **GitHub token support in the agent** (#77): with `GITHUB_TOKEN` set, `nekoagent upgrade` and `install.sh` are no longer blocked by GitHub's anonymous rate limits. The token is sent only to `api.github.com`. Downloads and API requests also gain timeouts and retries.  ## Fixed - **Live totals periodically dropped**: after each database flush, Total Download/Upload briefly under-counted one flush interval of traffic until the next push. This is fixed.  ## Upgrade notes - No data migration is needed, and the agent report protocol is unchanged.  See `CHANGELOG.md` / `CHANGELOG.en.md` for full details. 
```

## Install  bash uv tool install specify-cli --from git+https://github.com/github/spec-kit.git@v1.2.0 specify init my-project   ## What's Changed  - chore: bump version to 1.2.0 - fix: explain community submission title mismatches (#4946) - fix(workflows): confine workflow and step catalog cache writes (#4931) - docs: clarify delivery versus post-delivery spec persistence (#4945) - fix(bundles): allow newer packaged patch releases with warnings (#4942) - Update Applied Epistemic Engineering extension to v1.4.0 (#4941) - Update Applied Epistemic Engineering extension to v1.3.0 (#4936) - Persist project symlink policy at command boundaries (#4934) - [extension] Update DocGuard — CDD Enforcement extension to v0.44.3 (#4927) - fix(workflows): skip the right side of and/or when the left side decides (#4923) - Update OpenProject preset to v1.0.3 (#4928) - fix: narrow exceptions in get_speckit_version() from Exception to specific types (#3917) - Update adrkit extension to v0.1.5 (#4932) - Update OpenProject Integration extension to v0.2.0 (#4929) - feat(claude): add --no-model-invocation integration option (#4919) - Add built-in GitHub workflow step (#4905) - Add scoped FileHelper foundation for filesystem policy (#4908) - feat: add maintainer-triggered PR description assessment (#4902) - Update Architecture Governance preset to v0.6.2 (#4907) - Update Security Governance preset to v0.7.1 (#4903) - chore: release 1.1.3, begin 1.1.4.dev0 development (#4904)

### 2026-10-09T18:36:41Z [**symgraph/GhidrAssistMCP**](https://github.com/symgraph/GhidrAssistMCP)

```
This major release improves tool discovery, starts MCP from the Ghidra project manager, and adds project lifecycle control without requiring an open CodeBrowser. - Clearer tool names: 100 explicit tools, with 93 enabled by default. Dedicated operations such as get_pseudocode, get_disassembly, and get_pcode replace format selectors. - Unambiguous function tools: get_function_hash computes SHA-256, get_function_byte_pattern returns a masked byte pattern, and get_function_prototype returns the C declaration. - Application-wide runtime: MCP remains reachable after all CodeBrowsers close. Each browser’s panel shares the same configuration and request log. - Project controls: New create_project, open_project, save_project, and close_project tools include unsaved-change checks, verified saves, and protection against concurrent work. Opening a program can launch CodeBrowser. - Stronger execution contracts: Improved schema validation, error reporting, structured results, program selection, resource routing, caching, and asynchronous task cleanup. - Modernized dependencies: MCP Java SDK 2.0.1 and Jetty 12.1, packaged in an isolated runtime to avoid conflicts with Ghidra libraries. Validated against Ghidra 12.0 and 12.1, with 90 passing tests on each version. Breaking change: Consolidated tool names are no longer exposed over MCP. Reconnect your client or refresh its tool catalog after upgrading. Existing tool settings migrate to the new names. 
```

### 2026-10-11T17:24:44Z [**jasonwong1991/easy_proxies**](https://github.com/jasonwong1991/easy_proxies)

```
# v3.2.0 · 2026-10-12  ## 中文  ### 主要变化  - **主端口命名会话**：HTTP/SOCKS5 使用 `用户名.sessionID` 固定 TCP 上游节点，失败时按池策略切换；普通用户名保持原调度，旧来源 IP 粘性端口继续兼容。 - **更易用的面板**：会话 ID/接入链接生成与复制、绑定查询和重置、节点搜索/状态筛选、移动布局、按需分批展示，以及明确的操作失败反馈。 - **可靠性修复**：配置事务与原子保存、订阅失败回滚和手动刷新、生命周期并发、人工禁用保留、真实 HTTP 健康判断、稳定端口/VMess 身份迁移、GeoIP 转发/更新和管理认证。 - **交付与性能**：Go 1.26.9 工具链及兼容依赖更新，新增发布前质量门禁；会话查找、日志写入和大节点列表渲染优化。详见 [CHANGELOG](https://github.com/jasonwong1991/easy_proxies/blob/v3.2.0/CHANGELOG.md) 和 [审查报告](https://github.com/jasonwong1991/easy_proxies/blob/v3.2.0/docs/project-review-2026-10-11.md)。  ### 会话示例  listener 用户名为 `wang`、密码为示例 `1234` 时：  text http://wang:1234@127.0.0.1:2323            普通池调度 http://wang.account01:1234@127.0.0.1:2323  命名会话 account01 socks5h://wang.account01:1234@127.0.0.1:2323   `sessionID` 支持 1–64 个 `[A-Za-z0-9_-]` 字符；默认空闲 TTL 为 24h、容量为 10,000。固定的是上游节点，不保证服务商公网出口 IP 永不改变；既有连接不迁移，已发送的业务请求不重放。订阅刷新/核心重载可保留相同身份绑定，进程重启会清空会话。详细边界见 [会话路由指南](https://github.com/jasonwong1991/easy_proxies/blob/v3.2.0/docs/session-routing.md)。  ### 升级注意  1. 备份挂载配置目录并保留 `node_ports.json`；不要将格式示例节点当作可用代理。 2. Compose 可固定 `image: ghcr.io/jasonwong1991/easy_proxies:3.2.0`；该镜像发布任务完成后执行：     bash    docker compose pull easy_proxies    docker compose up -d --force-recreate easy_proxies      3. 源码需 **Go 1.26.9+**；Docker builder 固定为 1.26.9。可选协议与流量统计使用 README 中的完整生产标签。 4. **管理密码修改立即生效并撤销旧登录**，请重新登录后继续应用待处理设置。 5. 推荐目录挂载；单文件挂载兼容锁内原地写，但没有目录原子替换同等的断电/崩溃保障。外部编辑器不遵守锁协议时也不受事务保护。 6. 人工禁用节点现在只在到期或手动解除后恢复；任一订阅源失败/为空时保留整组旧订阅，并显示刷新错误。  交付延续 GitHub 源码归档和 GHCR `linux/amd64`、`linux/arm64` 镜像，本次不附独立二进制下载资产。  ### 验证范围  本地验证使用临时配置、合成节点/MMDB 和回环服务；没有使用真实订阅做公网可用性、吞吐或长期稳定性验证。发布工作流要求默认/生产标签测试、race、vet、UI/入口 fixture、可达漏洞扫描及 Linux amd64/arm64 构建通过，远端检查和镜像状态以 GitHub Actions 的实际结果为准。  ## English  ### Highlights  - **Named sessions on the main port**: `username.sessionID` keeps a TCP upstream node across HTTP/SOCKS5 connections, with pool-policy failover. The bare username retains ordinary scheduling; the legacy source-IP sticky port remains compatible. - **Usability**: session/link generation and copying, binding inspection/reset, node search/status filters, mobile layout, batched rendering, and explicit operation errors. - **Reliability**: transactional configuration saves, subscription rollback/manual refresh, lifecycle concurrency, preserved manual bans, actual HTTP probe validation, stable port/VMess identity migration, GeoIP handling, and live management authentication updates. - **Delivery/performance**: Go 1.26.9 and compatible dependency updates, pre-publication quality gates, faster session lookup/log writes, and lighter large-node views. See the [changelog](https://github.com/jasonwong1991/easy_proxies/blob/v3.2.0/CHANGELOG.md) and [review report](https://github.com/jasonwong1991/easy_proxies/blob/v3.2.0/docs/project-review-2026-10-11.md).  ### Upgrade notes  - Back up the mounted configuration directory and retain `node_ports.json`. Pin Docker to `ghcr.io/jasonwong1991/easy_proxies:3.2.0`, then pull/recreate after its image publication job completes using the commands above. - Source builds require **Go 1.26.9+**; Docker uses 1.26.9. Use the README's production tags for optional protocols/traffic statistics. - Management password changes apply immediately and revoke existing logins: sign in again before applying pending changes. - Prefer directory mounts for atomic replacement. Individual-file mounts use a locked in-place fallback with weaker crash/power-loss guarantees; external editors that ignore the lock protocol are outside transaction protection. - Named-session defaults are 24h idle TTL and 10,000 bindings; IDs accept 1–64 `[A-Za-z0-9_-]` characters. `http://wang.account01:1234@127.0.0.1:2323` uses the configured listener password, not a separately registered account. Bindings follow node identity across refresh/reload but are cleared on process restart. - Sessions fix an upstream node, not its provider's public exit IP. Existing connections are not migrated and application requests are not replayed. Manual bans now remain until expiry/manual release; a failed/empty subscription provider keeps the previous complete subscription set.  Delivery continues through GitHub source archives and GHCR `linux/amd64`/`linux/arm64` images; this release does not attach standalone binary assets.  ### Validation scope  Local validation uses temporary configuration, synthetic nodes/MMDB, and loopback services—not live subscriptions, public throughput, or long-running production soak tests. The publication workflow gates image building on default/production-tag tests, race/vet, UI/entrypoint fixtures, reachable vulnerability checks, and Linux amd64/arm64 builds. GitHub Actions records the actual remote check/image outcomes.   ## 发布验证 / Publication verification  - 发布提交 / Release commit: [88e146f](https://github.com/jasonwong1991/easy_proxies/commit/88e146fb65433fca9f4fc59d93aa4ed2535d68a1). - [main 工作流 / main workflow](https://github.com/jasonwong1991/easy_proxies/actions/runs/38158499335)：质量门禁及 Docker 构建/推送成功。 - [v3.2.0 工作流 / tag workflow](https://github.com/jasonwong1991/easy_proxies/actions/runs/38159219973)：质量门禁及 Docker 构建/推送成功。 - 默认/完整生产标签、race、vet、21 项 UI/入口 fixture、双架构编译、可达漏洞扫描通过。扫描无已知可达漏洞；仍有未导入的 module-only OpenPGP 公告，不代表依赖不存在任何安全公告。 - 已核验 GHCR `:3.2.0` 与 `:3.2` 相同镜像索引，包含 `linux/amd64` 和 `linux/arm64`；两平台 OCI revision 均匹配发布提交。`:latest` 由同一提交的 main 工作流更新。 - Registry index digest: `sha256:9befd1fe50c065a4f6c587de4b8c1cc5dfe493edc594a9ed8262ddd2d2ec2c64`.  Both remote workflows passed checks and image publication. Registry manifests and both platform revision labels were verified; this confirms build/publication, not public-subscription availability or long-running production behavior.  [完整变更 / Full comparison](https://github.com/jasonwong1991/easy_proxies/compare/v3.1.2...v3.2.0) 
```

# 更新内容  新增功能与改进：  1. 增强信令快速重连与连接恢复能力；桌面端配置修改无需重新连接信令服务。 2. Windows 安装版新增登录前远程控制支持，被控端可随系统启动，在尚未登录 Windows 时接受远程连接。 3. Linux 提供普通版与 Xfce 虚拟桌面版独立安装包，普通版不再强制安装 Xfce 和 Xvfb。 4. 被控端会话窗口改为紧凑的屏幕边缘状态栏。 5. 完善 iOS / Android 原生控制端，优化远程画面、触控与外接键鼠体验；Android 支持 H.264 / AV1 硬件解码，不支持时自动回退到软件解码。 6. 移动端新增画中画与后台会话保持支持。Android 在画中画不可用时使用前台服务保持连接。 7. 移动端新增悬浮虚拟鼠标，支持左键、右键、拖拽及滚轮操作，优化悬浮菜单和横屏布局。 8. 完善移动端会话操作：Android 新增文件收发、传输进度与接收文件保存；iOS 新增发送本机剪贴板文本入口；两端支持通过远端 Windows 服务发送 Ctrl+Alt+Del。 9. 优化 Android 平板和 iPad 的自适应布局，支持横竖屏与分屏调整，改善连接卡片、设置页面和弹窗展示。 10. iOS 和 Android 新增应用更新提示，支持首页更新标记、“关于”页面版本展示及手动检查更新。  问题修复:  1. 修复控制端异常断开后会话未及时清理，以及被控端断开后远程标签页未自动关闭的问题。 2. 修复桌面端自托管配置无法使用带路径或显式端口的服务器地址的问题，完善地址校验与保存反馈。 3. 修复 Windows 开机自启注册与旧启动项迁移问题，改善安装版登录后的自动启动行为。 4. 修复 Windows 管理员权限应用无法接收远程键鼠输入的问题，恢复远程快捷键并改善按键状态恢复。 5. 修复 Windows 锁屏、登录界面及安全桌面切换时的画面采集和输入异常。 6. 修复 Windows 隐私屏启动失败、解锁后无法自动恢复，以及锁屏界面出现重复鼠标光标的问题。 7. 修复 macOS 部分显示环境下视频延迟无法显示的问题，在缺少实际显示时间时提供带标记的估算值。 8. 修复 iOS 悬浮控件稳定性及模拟器视频解码问题，改善 Android 画面缩放、平移与鼠标光标显示的一致性。  ---  New Features and Improvements:  1. Improved signaling reconnection speed and connection recovery. Desktop configuration changes no longer require reconnecting to the signaling service. 2. Added remote access before Windows sign-in for the installed version. The host can start with Windows and accept remote connections before a user signs in. 3. Added separate standard and Xfce virtual desktop packages for Linux. The standard package no longer requires Xfce or Xvfb. 4. Replaced the host session window with a compact status bar at the screen edge. 5. Improved the native iOS / Android clients, including remote video, touch input, and external keyboard and mouse support. Android supports H.264 / AV1 hardware decoding with automatic software fallback when unsupported. 6. Added Picture in Picture and background session support on mobile. Android uses a foreground service to maintain connections when Picture in Picture is unavailable. 7. Added a floating virtual mouse on mobile, supporting left-click, right-click, dragging, and scrolling. Improved floating menus and landscape layouts. 8. Expanded mobile session controls: Android now supports sending and receiving files, transfer progress, and saving received files; iOS adds an action to send local clipboard text. Both clients support sending Ctrl+Alt+Del through the remote Windows service. 9. Improved adaptive layouts on Android tablets and iPad, including portrait, landscape, and split-screen support, with better connection cards, settings pages, and dialogs. 10. Added app update notifications on iOS and Android, including home-screen update indicators, version information on the About page, and manual update checks.  Bug Fixes:  1. Fixed sessions not being cleaned up promptly after unexpected controller disconnections, and remote session tabs remaining open after the host disconnects. 2. Fixed desktop self-hosted server configuration rejecting addresses with paths or explicit ports. Improved address validation and save feedback. 3. Fixed Windows autostart registration and migration of legacy startup entries, improving automatic startup after sign-in for the installed version. 4. Fixed remote keyboard and mouse input in Windows applications running with administrator privileges. Restored remote keyboard shortcuts and improved keyboard state recovery. 5. Fixed screen capture and input issues when switching between the Windows lock screen, sign-in screen, and secure desktop. 6. Fixed Windows privacy screen startup failures, failure to restore the privacy screen automatically after unlocking, and duplicate cursors on the lock screen. 7. Fixed missing video latency readings in some macOS display environments. Clearly labeled estimates are now provided when actual presentation timestamps are unavailable. 8. Fixed stability issues with iOS floating controls and video decoding in the iOS simulator. Improved consistency between Android and iOS zooming, panning, and cursor display. 
```

### 2026-10-09T08:25:08Z [**Gaurav-Gosain/tuios**](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios)

```
tuios 0.9.2 is a fix release with a security update. If you run tuios-web, update now. This build uses Go 1.26.9 and x/net v0.60.0, which fix a set of HTTP/2 and net/http advisories. It also fixes the macOS Option key on composing terminals and non-US layouts, keeps your terminal's screen and scrollback when you quit, draws images correctly in VS Code and Netcatty, and fixes herdr plugins on Windows.  The full list is at [tuios.dev/releases](https://tuios.dev/releases).  ## Install  bash brew install tuios                     # Homebrew (macOS, Linux) yay -S tuios-bin                       # AUR (tuios-web-bin for the web server) nix run github:Gaurav-Gosain/tuios     # Nix curl -fsSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/main/install.sh | bash go install github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/cmd/tuios@v0.9.2 docker run -it --rm ghcr.io/gaurav-gosain/tuios:v0.9.2   A binary installed by the script or from an archive updates with `tuios update`.  ## Security  - **Go 1.26.9 and x/net v0.60.0.** tuios and tuios-web now build with Go 1.26.9 and golang.org/x/net v0.60.0. These fix the net/http, HTTP/2, crypto/tls and os advisories GO-2026-6603 to GO-2026-6617. One of them, GO-2026-6617, lets a client crash an HTTP/2 server through a race in the HPACK encoder. tuios-web is an HTTP server, so update it first ([#593](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/593)). - **Build from source.** go.mod now has `toolchain go1.26.9`. If you build tuios yourself, use Go 1.26.9 or later.  ## New  - **macOS Option keys and keyboard layouts.** Two new keys in `[keybindings]` control how tuios reads Option ([#578](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/578), fixes [#566](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/issues/566) and points 1 and 3 of [#575](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/issues/575)).   - `option_glyphs = "type"` sends a character that Option composes to the pane. Use it if you type characters with Option, such as the right Option key in WezTerm. Before, right Option and 8 typed `•`, and tuios read it as `opt+8` and switched to workspace 8. The default, `"bind"`, works as before.   - `keyboard_layout = "other"` turns off the US aliases for shifted digits, such as `alt+&` for `alt+shift+7`. Set it on AZERTY, QWERTZ and other layouts. Before, Option and the 1 key on French AZERTY ran `move_and_follow_7`. The default is `"us"`.   - A key that the terminal reports through the Kitty keyboard protocol uses the layout in that report.   - A binding you set, such as `opt+&`, now always wins over a US alias.   - `tuios keybinds explain` lists the bindings that a key runs through a US alias, and says how to turn the aliases off.   - Docs: [Shifted digits and AZERTY](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/blob/main/docs/KEYBINDINGS.md#shifted-digits-and-azerty) and [One Option key for typing](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/blob/main/docs/KEYBINDINGS.md#one-option-key-for-typing). - **Links can use an open ssh master.** The daemon's links never ask for a password, a passphrase or a code. A link and `tuios hosts test` now use an ssh master that is already open in `$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR/tuios/cm`, so you can link a machine that needs a password or a second factor. With no master open, ssh connects as before. The link does not use the folder when other users can open it, when your ssh config gives the host its own `ControlPath`, or when the host forwards the agent ([#577](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/577)). Docs: [CONFIGURATION.md](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/blob/main/docs/CONFIGURATION.md). - **Ten session colours.** Sessions now take their automatic colour from ten hues, up from six. On a theme that shows ten different hues, ten sessions get ten colours. Two sessions with the same colour are not put in rows next to each other on the rail. On a theme that paints two hues the same, such as one_dark, the pool keeps only the hues you can tell apart. A colour you set with `set-session-accent` does not change ([#453](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/453)). Thanks to @davidreuss.  ## Fixes  - **Your screen after you quit.** tuios no longer clears your terminal's screen and scrollback when you quit or detach. The lines from before tuios started are back on screen, and you can scroll up to them. tuios now sends a soft reset (DECSTR) and turns off each mode it turned on, in place of a full reset (RIS) ([#592](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/592), [discussion #588](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/discussions/588)). - **Images in VS Code and Netcatty.** A terminal built on xterm.js leaves a picture behind when tuios moves or clears it. In Netcatty each scroll left a trail, and `clear` left a grey box. tuios now finds these terminals and draws pane images as block glyphs there. It reads the XTVERSION answer, also when it comes late or over `tuios ssh`, and `TERM_PROGRAM=vscode`. `TUIOS_KITTY_GRAPHICS=1` and `TUIOS_SIXEL_GRAPHICS=1` turn the graphics protocols on again. An image sent with the kitty protocol, such as `chafa -f kitty`, does not show on these terminals yet ([#583](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/583), fixes [#567](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/issues/567)). - **Reattach.** A reattach now keeps state that no cell shows. Before, a client looked right until a program used that state ([#580](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/580)).   - The cursor that DECSC saved, on both screens. After a reattach, a shell's cursor went to the top left when vim quit.   - Cells a program protected with DECSCA. A selective erase after a reattach erased them.   - The character that REP (`CSI b`) repeats.   - An open OSC 8 link on the ghostty build. Text printed in the link after a reattach was not a link.   - A client bigger than the snapshot is sized to the snapshot. A line that wrapped at the last column then wraps at the same place. - **Terminal emulation.** The pure Go emulator now supports DECSCA protected cells. It restores the cursor and the character sets when a program leaves the alternate screen with 1049, as xterm and libghostty do. Before, the character sets of a full-screen program stayed in force after it quit ([#580](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/580)). - **Windows.** These fixes are for herdr plugins and the daemon on Windows ([#581](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/581), fixes [#579](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/issues/579)).   - herdr plugins can open panes. The daemon now makes `herdr.exe` as a hardlink, or as a copy when the binary is on another drive.   - A plugin program named without `.exe`, such as `bin/<name>`, is found through PATHEXT.   - A plugin's processes stop when the plugin stops or the daemon ends. A program that a plugin command opens, such as an editor, keeps running.   - `tuios kill-server` asks the daemon to stop, so the daemon saves its state and removes its sockets. Before, it ended the daemon at once and then timed out. It also reaches a daemon that you started over OpenSSH.   - CI now runs these tests on Windows. A plugin that opens a pane and `kill-server` against a live daemon are not yet tested on a Windows desktop. Please report what you see in [#579](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/issues/579). - **tuios-web.** tuios-web moves to sip v0.9.0 and then v0.9.1 ([#584](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/584), [#594](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/594)).   - The fonts are WOFF2 and static text is gzipped. A cold load is about 4.4 MB, down from 11.7 MB.   - The vtgl renderer draws underlines under spaces.   - The vtgl renderer puts the grid in the right place. Before, it drew the grid about 200 px too low on tuios.dev/learn.  ## Learn tuios  The guided tour at [tuios.dev/learn](https://tuios.dev/learn) fits its output to the pane.  - `fastfetch` picks the largest layout that fits the pane. `neofetch` still works as another name for it ([#595](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/595)). - `ls` prints names in columns and never breaks a name across two lines. `help`, `git`, `colors`, `fortune`, `cowsay`, `top` and the sample tables also fit the pane ([#599](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/599)). - `?renderer=vtgl` loads the vtgl renderer ([#582](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/582)).  ## Upgrading  ### The daemon  The daemon protocol did not change, so a v0.9.1 daemon works with a v0.9.2 client. The new snapshot fields are optional in both directions. The reattach fixes, the ssh master links and the Windows plugin and `kill-server` fixes are in the daemon. To get them, restart the daemon:  bash tuios kill-server   This closes every pane in every session, so save your work first.  ### Config  Two new keys, both in `[keybindings]`. Their defaults keep the old behaviour.  toml [keybindings] keyboard_layout = "us"   # us, other option_glyphs = "bind"   # bind, type   ### Session colours  Each session's automatic colour can change once, because the pool now has ten hues.  ### tuios-web  sip no longer serves the `/static/fonts/*.ttf` URLs. The fonts are at `/static/fonts/*.woff2`. If a page or proxy of yours loads the TTF files, change it to the WOFF2 files.  ## Contributors  Thank you to everyone who sent a pull request for this release:  - @davidreuss: ten session colours, with no two the same next to each other on the rail ([#453](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/453))  And thanks to everyone who reported bugs: @dominionthedev for the composed Option characters ([#566](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/issues/566)), @florian-guily for the AZERTY keys ([#575](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/issues/575)), @LovesAsuna for the image trails ([#567](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/issues/567)), @EricSanzLuna for herdr plugins on Windows ([#579](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/issues/579)), and @mhmdar for the cleared screen on exit ([#588](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/discussions/588)).  ---  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/compare/v0.9.1...v0.9.2  ---  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/compare/v0.9.1...v0.9.2 
```

### 2026-10-12T07:27:58Z [**longbridge/gpui-kit**](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit)

```
GPUI Kit v0.7.3 replaces v0.7.2, which is yanked: it contains every v0.7.2 change and moves GPUI to the gpui-pre 0.3.9 snapshot. Upgrade from v0.7.1 or v0.7.2 directly to v0.7.3.  This release adds a readonly Diff viewer with a Git history example, an optional GPUI Fast backend, and configurable dialog entrances. It refreshes TextView typography and improves text selection, Editor completions, tables, menus, tooltips and accessibility, and stops several per-frame state writes that made long scrolling views rebuild.  ## New components and runtime  - **Diff**: display application-supplied unified or Git patches with the virtualized, readonly `Diff` view. `DiffFile::parse` prepares each file and one `DiffState` shows them all, with Unified or Split layout (`DiffMode`), syntax highlighting, folding, change navigation, inline word or character emphasis, soft wrapping, annotations, and two-way or diff3 conflict display. Applications own patch loading, review comments and saving. ([#3378](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3378)) - **Git history example**: `examples/tig` is a desktop Git history browser built on Diff, with a resizable commit and file sidebar, Split layout and a settings menu; the Diff review APIs it needed are complete. ([#3388](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3388)) - **GPUI snapshot**: exact snapshot dependencies are updated to `=0.3.9` (zed@93d7456), and the optional GPUI Fast backend requires `gpui-fast` 0.1.7 on the same snapshot. ([#3459](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3459)) - **GPUI Fast backend**: enable the optional `gpui-fast` feature (`gpui-kit = { version = "0.7", features = ["gpui-fast"] }`) to run on GPUI Fast instead of the default gpui-pre backend. Imports and macros are unchanged, and Windows applications embed a single manifest. ([#3375](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3375), [#3380](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3380)) - **Fewer rebuilds**: selectable text, the text selection layer, `Root`, inputs, `TextViewState` and resizable panels no longer write state on frames where nothing changed, so retained views under GPUI Fast stay on their scroll layer. ([#3407](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3407), [#3418](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3418))  ## Editor and text input  - **On-demand completions**: dispatch the Editor-only `ShowCompletions` action or call `show_completions` to request suggestions from a command, menu or application shortcut; no default shortcut is bound. ([#3411](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3411)) - **Programmatic multi-cursor**: `InputState::add_selection` adds a selection while keeping the existing ones, enabling features such as select all occurrences. ([#3403](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3403)) - **Context menus**: an input keeps its selection and focus ring while its context menu is open, and submenus inherit the parent menu's `action_context`, so actions such as Select All reach the source Textarea. Custom `on_context_menu` builders now run after the input is released and can read its cursor and text. ([#3382](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3382), [#3433](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3433)) - Fixed completion and code-action popups lagging one frame behind the caret, and auto-grow Textareas collapsing to `min_rows` after `set_auto_grow` changes the limits. ([#3412](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3412), [#3430](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3430))  ## Markdown and text rendering  - **Typography**: TextView headings build hierarchy with weight, sizes and spacing scale with the inherited font size, and lists, code, links and tables are more restrained. See Behavior changes. ([#3371](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3371)) - **Selection**: mouse selections include block `MarkdownPlugin` content as whole blocks, completed selections survive style, zoom and width changes, and the selection paints under the glyphs so opaque selection colors keep text readable. ([#3373](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3373), [#3390](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3390), [#3421](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3421), [#3439](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3439)) - **Tables**: narrow columns size from measured text width and stay on one line, and rounded table frames keep clean corners at any horizontal scroll offset. `RoundedFrameCover` is available for application frames with their own rounded scroll viewports. ([#3399](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3399), [#3309](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3309), [#3400](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3400)) - Fixed inline `<mark>` highlights in Markdown, including `color` and `background-color` attributes. ([#3425](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3425))  ## Components  - **Dialog**: choose an entrance with `entrance(DialogEntrance::Fade)`, `FadeSlide`, `None` or the default `SlideDown` on `Dialog` and `AlertDialog`; `AlertDialog::margin_top` moves an alert off the default top offset. ([#3405](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3405), [#3404](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3404)) - **Tooltip**: install `TooltipDefaults` to set the application-wide show delay and grace period, or override one trigger with `tooltip_show_delay`. ([#3376](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3376)) - **DataTable**: `header_row_height` sets header rows apart from body rows; the sort icon is a labelled button; `visible_rows_changed` reports one-row and empty tables; a right-clicked cell's outline clears on the next click, exposed through `TableState::right_clicked_cell`. ([#3420](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3420), [#3396](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3396), [#3397](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3397), [#3413](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3413)) - **ContextMenu**: a long press opens the menu on touch devices, and the menu takes focus before its first frame, avoiding a debug-build abort with accessibility active. ([#3393](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3393), [#3422](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3422)) - **TabBar**: clicking a clipped tab scrolls it into view with the next tab peeking. ([#3419](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3419)) - **Dock**: closing the focused tab hands focus to the tab that replaces it. ([#3409](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3409)) - **DropdownButton**: `menu_accessibility_label` names the caret-only menu trigger, with a localized "More options" default. ([#3410](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3410)) - **Settings**: focus rings stay inside the list viewport. ([#3394](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3394)) - **UI tests**: `Progress`, `ProgressCircle` and `TitleBar` can be found with `TestWindowExt::find`. ([#3383](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3383))  ## Documentation  - **Focus and dialogs**: the guides explain recovering focus when the focused element stops rendering, and the `open_dialog` builder and footer contracts. ([#3386](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3386), [#3406](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3406))  ## Behavior changes  TextView renders with new default typography. Heading sizes follow the inherited font size, the paragraph gap is `0.75rem` (was `1rem`), and list, inline code, link and table cell styling change. The paragraph gap, headings and code blocks can be restored through the existing style hooks. ([#3371](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3371))  diff  TextViewStyle::default() +    .paragraph_gap(rems(1.))   Table column widths in the default wrap layout change for most Markdown tables. A drag that starts and ends inside the same block plugin no longer selects it. ([#3399](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3399), [#3390](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3390))  While a context menu is open, focus that the application moves elsewhere is no longer pulled back to the menu. ([#3422](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3422))  `TableDelegate::visible_rows_changed` now fires with `0..1` for a one-row table and `0..0` when the rows drop to none, and its end never passes `rows_count`. ([#3397](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3397))  `TooltipRequest::placement` is deprecated in favor of `with_placement`. ([#3376](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3376))  diff - TooltipRequest::new(bounds, build).placement(Placement::Right) + TooltipRequest::new(bounds, build).with_placement(Placement::Right)   Thank you to everyone who contributed code, documentation, testing, and feedback!  ## What's Changed * text_view: Improve default typography by @madcodelife in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3371 * text_view: Include block plugins in mouse selections by @lurenjia534 in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3373 * kit: Add optional gpui-fast backend by @huacnlee in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3375 * tooltip: Make show delay and grace period configurable with `TooltipDefaults` by @madcodelife in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3376 * docs: Explain recovering focus when the focused element stops rendering by @AncientPixel in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3386 * diff: Add readonly patch viewer by @huacnlee in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3378 * kit: Use one Windows manifest with GPUI Fast by @huacnlee in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3380 * test: Make Progress and TitleBar findable in UI tests by @AncientPixel in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3383 * input: Preserve selection, focus ring and context menu actions by @lurenjia534 in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3382 * diff: Add a Git history example and complete review APIs by @huacnlee in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3388 * text_view: Ignore gestures that stay inside one block plugin by @ylinwind in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3390 * text_view: Round ### 2026-10-08T09:17:41Z [**nickrunning/wechat-selkies**](https://github.com/nickrunning/wechat-selkies)

```
## Changes  - Restore Issue validation and cleanup permissions for labels, comments, and issue updates. - Record the actual QQ fallback download URL together with its matching version and SHA256 hash. Prefer the last successful package before using the backup version. - Explicitly trigger image builds after automated package updates, serialize publishing, and verify package SHA256 hashes before installation. - Add nine upstream detection regression tests and update the automation documentation.  ## Validation  The release workflow passed. Full and minimal images were built for linux/amd64 and linux/arm64 and published to GitHub Container Registry and Docker Hub. Upstream detection and all nine regression tests also passed on GitHub Actions.  ## 🐳 Docker Images  ### Full (WeChat + QQ + File Manager) **GitHub Container Registry:** bash docker pull ghcr.io/nickrunning/wechat-selkies:0.0.17 docker pull ghcr.io/nickrunning/wechat-selkies:latest   **Docker Hub** (if enabled): bash docker pull nickrunning/wechat-selkies:0.0.17 docker pull nickrunning/wechat-selkies:latest   ### Minimal (WeChat only) bash docker pull ghcr.io/nickrunning/wechat-selkies:minimal   ## 🚀 Quick Start **Full:** bash docker run -it -p 3001:3001 -v ./config:/config --device /dev/dri:/dev/dri ghcr.io/nickrunning/wechat-selkies:latest   **Minimal:** bash docker run -it -p 3001:3001 -v ./config:/config --device /dev/dri:/dev/dri ghcr.io/nickrunning/wechat-selkies:minimal   Then visit: https://localhost:3001   **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/nickrunning/wechat-selkies/compare/v0.0.16...v0.0.17 
```

### 2026-10-07T22:07:42Z [**TheBeef/WhippyTerm**](https://github.com/TheBeef/WhippyTerm)

```
## Version 2.4.0.0 -- 05 Oct 2026 ### Enhancements to binary mode  * [Polish] Added a link to the web site to the help menu    - A new menu option (Help&rarr;Goto WhippyTerm Web Site) opens whippyterm.com in your browser.  * [Bug fix] Windows: removing an open com port was not noticed    - If a serial port was removed from the system while it was open (for example unplugging a USB serial adapter) the connection could still show as open.  The serial driver now checks that the port is still there and reports the connection as disconnected when it is gone.  * [Bug fix] Linux: UDP server reuse address / reuse port options were not applied correctly    - The two options were being combined into one request to the system. They are now set one at a time.  * [New Feature] New options have been added to scroll to be bottom to rx/tx    - Two new options have been added to the settings.  Under Display there is now a Scroll To Bottom with two checkboxes.  One will have the screen scroll to the bottom when a byte is sent and the other scrolls to the bottom when a byte is received.  If both a checked then the screen will be scrolled to the bottom on send and receive.  If neither are check then the screen will not be scrolled back to the bottom when bytes are sent or received (it will still stay at the bottom if it was already there)  * [Bug fix] Local echo was showing file transfer data    - When local echo was turned on and you uploaded or downloaded a file the bytes sent by the file transfer protocol were being echoed to the screen.  File transfer data is no longer local echoed.  * [New Feature] Added an option to highlight a tab when new data arrives in the background    - You can now choose to display an icon beside the tab name when new data arrives on an inactive tab. This allows you to keep multiple tabs open while easily spotting which ones need attention.  * [Enhancement] Added a new settings to let you set the auto lap time    - The auto lap in the stop watch was set to a fixed 500ms of quite before the auto lap will trigger.  You can now select how long to wait in settings.  * [New Feature] Add insert horizontal rule to toolbar    - Added a toolbar button to insert horizontal rule.  * [Enhancement] Changed the defaults for horizontal rule    - Changed the default settings for the horizontal rule.  It now defaults to override and set to green.  This makes it stand out more.  * [New Feature] Added find in text    - A search the text panel has been added.  You can select it from the edit menu, or with the default key bindings of SHIFT+CTRL+F.  The search supports whole words, match case, and searching from the top of the scroll back buffer or top of the screen.  You can search forward or backward and the system adds past searches to the session data. In binary mode you can also search using a hex string or normal text.  * [New Feature] The bottom hex dump panels now support the same bytes per line/divider lines as binary mode    - The bottom panel hex dumps now support setting the number of bytes are displayed on a line.  A divider line can all be set to group bytes together (all the same size).  The color and thickness of these divider lines and also be set.  This can be found under: Settings->Panels->Hex Display->Hex Dumps  * [New Feature] In binary mode now includes divider lines    - In binary mode settings you can set a divider line every x bytes.  A line will be drawn in the selected color grouping the bytes together.  The thickness of the line can also be selected.  This can be found under: Settings->Terminal->Data Processing->Set Hex Dump Appearance  * [New Feature] In binary mode you can now select number of bytes per line    - In binary mode settings you can now set the number of bytes perline.  This can be found under: Settings->Terminal->Data Processing->Set Hex Dump Appearance  * [Bug fix] In binary mode with a fixed size window the horz scroll bar had wrong size    - When you used a fixed size term (like 80 chars) in binary mode the bottom horz scroll bar would still use the full screen width instead of the fixed term size.  Fixed  * [Bug fix] In binary mode with a fixed size window selection did not work    - When you used a fixed size term (like 80 chars) the selection wasn't taking the edge into account and so would select incorrectly  * [Bug fix] Rewrote sound playback system    - Was using the Qt sound playback system but this turned out to be unreliable and inconsistent between platforms.  It has been rewritten to use platform API calls instead.  * [Polish] Fixed a number background color issues    - The background color would act odd when starting from a new connection/reset term/clear screen.  It has all been cleaned up.  * [Polish] Newline now fills the line with the background color    - When you do a newline it will fill the rest of the line with the current background color.  * [Polish] Fixed color below text area    - When a full line doesn't fit below the text area, that area needs to be filled with something.  It was always being filled with the default background color.  It is now filled with the active background color or the color of the last line drawn.  * [Polish] More work on the scroll lock button    - More work has been added to trying to get the scroll lock key to behave correcly.  * [New Feature] Added a better indictor of cursor keys mode    - When you toggle the cursor keys mode (scrolllock/insert/etc) the system would change the shape of the cursor in the window.  However if the cursor was off the bottom there was no way to see what mode you where in.  A new indicator box has been added to show it's state in the top right corner of the display.  * [New Feature] Added check for new version system    - Added a new menu option (help->Check for new version) to go out to the WhippyTerm web site and check for a new version. This can also added settings to be able to auto check for new versions on startup/daily/monthly/yearly.  * [Bug fix] Fixed a number of small bugs    - More scanning found a number of small bugs.  Fixed them.  * [Bug fix] Found a number of problems with the ANSI clearing    - The ANSI processor was not handling the ESC[J and ESC[K commands correctly.  It was trying to clear 0 size areas which resulted in nothing changing.  * [Functionally fix] Custom connection settings where confusing    - When a connection was using custom settings and the user selected to change the settings (global) the changes would not get applied (because it was setting the global, but the custom settings override it). A new dialog has been added to warn the user they are using custom settings when they select the global settings.  The user can select to edit the global settings or the connection settings. 
```

### 2026-10-13T06:11:02Z [**0xZhangKe/Fread**](https://github.com/0xZhangKe/Fread)

```
## 🎨 Improvements  - **Long posts:** Preserve expanded and collapsed states across UI updates, reducing the need to expand posts again. - **Image viewer:** Add translucent backgrounds to the download and image description buttons for better visibility. Add localized accessibility labels. - **Search:** Hide the divider while the search bar is active for a cleaner interface. - **AI-generated alt text:** Show a clear message, including the model name, when the selected model does not support image input. - **Post language detection (Android):** Switch to Apache OpenNLP with a bundled model for offline detection. Lower the minimum text length from 40 to 10 characters so shorter posts can receive language suggestions.  ## 🐛 Bug Fixes  - Fix a potential crash when a Mastodon mention notification has no associated post. - Fix a crash when parsing certain Bluesky post links containing unexpected account identifiers. - Correct content type handling in Bluesky screens to prevent other content types from being routed incorrectly. - Add missing rules required for Android release builds.  ## 🛠️ Build and Dependencies  - Update Kotlin, Compose Multiplatform, KSP, Android Gradle Plugin, and Firebase dependencies. - Remove lingua-kmp and use OpenNLP for Android language detection. - Improve Android signing configuration so development builds work without a local signing configuration file. Thanks to [[@realstealthninja](https://github.com/realstealthninja)](https://github.com/realstealthninja) for the contribution ([[#165](https://github.com/0xZhangKe/Fread/pull/165)](https://github.com/0xZhangKe/Fread/pull/165)).  **Full Changelog:** [[1.10.0…1.10.2](https://github.com/0xZhangKe/Fread/compare/1.10.0...1.10.2)](https://github.com/0xZhangKe/Fread/compare/1.10.0...1.10.2) 
```
