# 发布时间 * 2026-10-12 11:40 GMT+0800  # 使用手册 * [WIKI & SKILL](https://github.com/vernesong/OpenClash/blob/dev/.github/skills/openclash-user-guide/SKILL.md)  # 安装命令  SHELL # [iptables for ipk] opkg update opkg install bash iptables dnsmasq-full curl ca-bundle ipset ip-full iptables-mod-tproxy iptables-mod-extra ruby ruby-yaml kmod-tun kmod-inet-diag unzip luci-compat luci luci-base curl -L --retry 2 https://api.github.com/repos/vernesong/OpenClash/releases/latest -o /tmp/openclash_version [ -f "/tmp/openclash_version" ] && download_url=$(cat /tmp/openclash_version | jsonfilter -e '@.assets[*].browser_download_url' | grep '\.ipk$') && curl -L --retry 2 "$download_url" -o /tmp/openclash.ipk || echo "OpenClash last version get failed" [ -f "/tmp/openclash.ipk" ] && opkg install /tmp/openclash.ipk || echo "OpenClash download failed"   SHELL # [iptables for apk] apk update apk add bash iptables dnsmasq-full curl ca-bundle ipset ip-full iptables-mod-tproxy iptables-mod-extra ruby ruby-yaml kmod-tun kmod-inet-diag unzip luci-compat luci luci-base curl -L --retry 2 https://api.github.com/repos/vernesong/OpenClash/releases/latest -o /tmp/openclash_version [ -f "/tmp/openclash_version" ] && download_url=$(cat /tmp/openclash_version | jsonfilter -e '@.assets[*].browser_download_url' | grep '\.apk$') && curl -L --retry 2 "$download_url" -o /tmp/openclash.apk || echo "OpenClash last version get failed" [ -f "/tmp/openclash.apk" ] && apk add -q --force-overwrite --clean-protected --allow-untrusted /tmp/openclash.apk || echo "OpenClash download failed"   SHELL # [nftables for ipk] opkg update opkg install bash dnsmasq-full curl ca-bundle ip-full ruby ruby-yaml kmod-tun kmod-inet-diag unzip kmod-nft-tproxy luci-compat luci luci-base curl -L --retry 2 https://api.github.com/repos/vernesong/OpenClash/releases/latest -o /tmp/openclash_version [ -f "/tmp/openclash_version" ] && download_url=$(cat /tmp/openclash_version | jsonfilter -e '@.assets[*].browser_download_url' | grep '\.ipk$') && curl -L --retry 2 "$download_url" -o /tmp/openclash.ipk || echo "OpenClash last version get failed" [ -f "/tmp/openclash.ipk" ] && opkg install /tmp/openclash.ipk || echo "OpenClash download failed"   SHELL # [nftables for apk] apk update apk add bash dnsmasq-full curl ca-bundle ip-full ruby ruby-yaml kmod-tun kmod-inet-diag unzip kmod-nft-tproxy luci-compat luci luci-base curl -L --retry 2 https://api.github.com/repos/vernesong/OpenClash/releases/latest -o /tmp/openclash_version [ -f "/tmp/openclash_version" ] && download_url=$(cat /tmp/openclash_version | jsonfilter -e '@.assets[*].browser_download_url' | grep '\.apk$') && curl -L --retry 2 "$download_url" -o /tmp/openclash.apk || echo "OpenClash last version get failed" [ -f "/tmp/openclash.apk" ] && apk add -q --force-overwrite --clean-protected --allow-untrusted /tmp/openclash.apk || echo "OpenClash download failed"    * 启动前请先在插件设置页面的版本更新标签下确认`内核编译版本`，您也可以手动下载 [Clash内核](https://github.com/MetaCubeX/mihomo/releases) 后解压到 `/etc/openclash/core/`文件夹，并将二进制文件命名为`clash_meta`  # 常见问题 * 路由不建议开启IPV6和相关服务，目前反馈的网络连接问题多数与它有关。 > [连接异常时请按照此页步骤先进行检查，解决不了再反馈](https://github.com/vernesong/OpenClash/wiki/网络连接异常时排查原因) * 浏览器开启安全DNS，会导致分流异常，请关闭！ * 部分软件会修改设备HOSTS，会导致分流异常，请注意检查！ * 控制面板打开空白时请尝试刷新浏览器缓存 * 默认代理路由本机流量，BT、PT下载等请尽量使用redir模式并注意进行流量规避（可在规则中将路由地址设置为直连） * 备份插件设置后可在配置文件管理页面上传压缩包进行还原  # 版本变化 * 首页添加初始引导 * Ruby 加载性能的优化，包括 ruby - uci 的读取和转换，缩短读取转换时间 * 主题颜色允许自定义 * 连通性检查页面添加常见服务解锁情况模式 * 默认禁用 tcp_fastopen_blackhole_timeout_sec ，防止旧版固件使用 TFO 异常 * TUN 模式默认启用GSO，默认使用 MIPS，相关模式、性能对比请查看 [性能实测数据](https://github.com/vernesong/OpenClash/blob/dev/.github/skills/openclash-user-guide/08-settings-mode-traffic.md#84-%E6%80%A7%E8%83%BD%E5%AE%9E%E6%B5%8B%E6%95%B0%E6%8D%AE) * 覆写模块加载时的安全限制 * 插件和内核更新时添加 sha256sum 检查 * 更新时 CDN 列表的自动远程同步 * 运行数据卡片添加折线视图 * 订阅添加时的步骤、视图指引强化，添加订阅转换模板预览，添加配置文件内容概览 * 覆写模块视图调整，添加 Google Play、Tailscale（解决本机代理时启用accept-dns可能导致的异常）、WifiCalling 默认模块 * 移除文件上传大小限制 * nginx 服务端的适配 * 页面交互细节、启动流程的增强和优化 * 已知错误和bug的修复  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/vernesong/OpenClash/compare/v0.47.156...v0.48.002 
```

```
## 更新日志 - **修复** 全屏切换时的卡顿问题 - **修复** "特别关注"的分页错乱 - **调整** "特别关注"顶部的Avatar区域默认按加入时间倒序排列 - **调整** 对重复下载做检查, 自动抛弃 - **调整** 播放器清晰度增加了记忆 - **调整**  历史记录的搜索使用官方接口`/x/web-interface/history/search?` - **调整** 所有页面改为统一的Fluent返回按钮 - **新功能** 适配视频分p, 在播放器右下角类似"番剧"的选集按钮 - **新功能** 适配Xbox手柄操作和鼠标侧键返回 - **新功能** 直播播放器添加录制和帧截图功能 - **新功能** 启动时的更新提醒   ## macOS arm64 说明 可能需要 macOS 27.0 最新版系统才能运行, 如有问题, 请提交issue  ## Windows x64  包体积大小说明  由于打包了115MB的`libmpv`与98MB的`FFmpeg`, 导致体积暴增, 请见谅, 如果已经自行安装, 请手动删除释放空间

### 2026-10-11T08:34:35Z [**nxtrace/NTrace-core**](https://github.com/nxtrace/NTrace-core)

```
## What's Changed  * 新增 `-k/--traceroute`，可显式选择传统 traceroute；`nexttrace-tiny` 新增可选 MTR TUI、报告和 RAW 模式 by @tsosunchia in https://github.com/nxtrace/NTrace-dev/pull/229 * 三个 flavor 均支持 MTR JSON：`--mtr --json` 输出 NDJSON，`-r/-w --json` 输出最终报告 by @tsosunchia in https://github.com/nxtrace/NTrace-dev/pull/230 * 新增 MTR 会话录制与离线回放；录制可与 TUI、报告、RAW、JSON 输出配合，回放不发起探测 by @tsosunchia in https://github.com/nxtrace/NTrace-dev/pull/237 * 扩展 MTR 统计列，新增几何均值与抖动指标；支持自定义列、TUI 列编辑，以及将当前统计保存为 TXT、JSON 或 HTML 报告 by @tsosunchia in https://github.com/nxtrace/NTrace-dev/pull/231, https://github.com/nxtrace/NTrace-dev/pull/239, https://github.com/nxtrace/NTrace-dev/pull/240 * 新增 `--doctor` 环境自检，在不发送探测包的情况下报告目标解析、源地址选择、路由预测和后端初始化结果 by @tsosunchia in https://github.com/nxtrace/NTrace-dev/pull/232 * 新增 Linux `--fwmark`，为本地 traceroute 和 MTR 探测设置策略路由标记；系统路由规则仍由用户配置 by @tsosunchia in https://github.com/nxtrace/NTrace-dev/pull/233 * 加强 deploy/MCP 请求限制：限制探测参数和登录正文大小，并设置 HTTP 读取与空闲连接超时 by @tsosunchia in https://github.com/nxtrace/NTrace-dev/pull/228 * 修复 MTR 与 WebUI 对零 RTT 成功响应的统计和显示问题 * 修正 `--tos` 策略路由选源与配置失败处理，并改进抓包过滤，避免无关 TOS 流量混入 by @tsosunchia in https://github.com/nxtrace/NTrace-dev/pull/236, https://github.com/nxtrace/NTrace-dev/pull/238 * 改进 Geo HTTP 连接与缓存复用、DN42 GeoFeed 前缀查询和 MTR 聚合快照处理，降低重复查找与热路径开销 by @tsosunchia in https://github.com/nxtrace/NTrace-dev/pull/218, https://github.com/nxtrace/NTrace-dev/pull/219, https://github.com/nxtrace/NTrace-dev/pull/220, https://github.com/nxtrace/NTrace-dev/pull/221 * 升级至 Go 1.27.1，macOS 发布构建最低版本提高至 13.0；更新 `quic-go`、`go-runewidth`、`miekg/dns`、`testify` 等依赖 by @tsosunchia in https://github.com/nxtrace/NTrace-dev/pull/216  额外的：   本版本尚未切换 `nexttrace` 和 `nexttrace-tiny` 的默认模式，默认仍为传统 traceroute。依赖传统 RAW 输出的调用可使用 `--traceroute --raw`；未来默认模式切换将另行公告。   MTR TUI 和 `--mtr --raw` 未指定 `-q` 时持续运行；report/wide 模式（包括与 `--raw` 组合）默认每跳探测 10 次。自动化调用请显式设置 `-q`。   Linux `--fwmark` 仅适用于本地 traceroute/MTR，NextTrace 不会修改系统路由规则；设置标记需要相应 Linux capability。   macOS 最低支持版本为 13.0。源码构建需要 Go 1.27.1+；对齐正式发布构建时使用 `GOEXPERIMENT=nojsonv2`。    **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/nxtrace/NTrace-core/compare/v1.7.3...v1.7.4 
```

### 2026-10-08T17:40:36Z [**charmbracelet/bubbletea**](https://github.com/charmbracelet/bubbletea)

# Hello OSC 7501  The exciting news in this release is support for the brand new [Program Status Protocol](https://www.superlogical.com/rex/docs/build/program-status), otherwise known as `OSC 7501`, released just a few days ago. The protocol is designed to allow terminal applications to report what they're doing (e.g. idle, working, waiting on the user, finished, or failed) and why.  The obvious case for the design is AI agents, but are lots of other use cases for this, and we’re excited to see what you build with it.  It’s subtle, but here’s [Crush](https://github.com/charmbracelet/crush) in Superlogical’s [Rex](https://www.superlogical.com/updates/public-testing-beginning) with the feature integrated (notice the dot in the first tab).  <p><img width="700" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/85680681-9397-456b-8283-4eed19635681" /></p>  ## Bugfixes  Also, this release brings some very important fixes done by @AdamMagued, @simpleqt and @GreedyC.  Cheers! Charm 🌞  ## Changelog ### New! * 41bc6265643a4709ad355581b01e6bb70b2ed8ea: feat: OSC 7501 (#1841) (@andrinoff) ### Fixed * 96d69d2f7eb182bb311be08fc56ed2c208239aff: fix(tty): fall back to default dimensions when terminal size is 0x0 (#1836) (@AdamMagued) * ff51ba4c51f85875761b15a64f9ab9aa0eaa9fa1: fix: prevent signal goroutine deadlock on shutdown (#1806) (@simpleqt) ### Docs * 33f6a40ec035111bc11d5fd5304cac1359af44e0: docs: fix command and environment godoc examples (#1833) (@GreedyC) ### Other stuff * f9df43c4f2c021d8740a2628d59cd810a862eb98: v2.1.0 (@andrinoff)  ---  <a href="https://charm.land/"><img alt="The Charm logo" src="https://stuff.charm.sh/charm-banner-next.jpg" width="400"></a>  Thoughts? Questions? We love hearing from you. Feel free to reach out on [X](https://x.com/charmcli), [Discord](https://charm.land/discord), [Slack](https://charm.land/slack), [The Fediverse](https://mastodon.social/@charmcli), [Bluesky](https://bsky.app/profile/charm.land).

### 2026-10-12T07:47:55Z [**drogonframework/drogon**](https://github.com/drogonframework/drogon)

```
### API changes list  - Add `HttpAppFramework::hasDbClient()` and `hasFastDbClient()` methods. (#2547)  - Add coroutine WebSocket controller API and integration tests. (#2523)  - Add `DROGON_USE_BOOST_UUID` option to use Boost.UUID instead of OS libuuid. (#2563)  - Add support for custom source and header file extensions in `drogon_ctl`. (#2604)  - Introduce `SqlFieldType` abstraction and improve MySQL result handling. (#2589)  ### Changed  - Improve static file cache expiry to use `staticFilesCacheTime_` instead of the epoch. (#2515)  - Add support for static `jsoncpp` library. (#2528)  - Support custom namespaces for `dg_ctl create model`. (#2539)  - Add release tag support for Docker image publishing.  - Respect application-set `closeConnection` in response processing. (#2566)  - Optimize `drogon_test` build speed. (#2565)  - Add batch test selection to the test runner.  - Add default headers to `HttpClient`. (#2594)  - Add database reconnect interval configuration for ORM. (#2611)  - Initialize SQLite pool connections. (#2610)  - Refactor `drogon_ctl` view generation and fix related issues. (#2603)  ### Fixed  - Fix `ConfigLoader::bytesSize` parsing values ending with `6`. (#2521)  - Fix SQLite CI/test issues. (#2522)  - Fix forwarding requests when the body is cached on disk. (#2525)  - Fix `drogon::utils::utf8Length`-related ORM generation compatibility issues. (#2524)  - Fix HTTP/1.1 streaming on close connection. (#2527)  - Fix connection buffer sizing for atomic requests. (#2532)  - Fix DST handling in ORM model date/datetime conversion. (#2536)  - Fix SQLite cleanup with cached statements. (#2549)  - Fix `HttpClient` not re-resolving hostnames on reconnect. (#2548)  - Fix ambiguous requests containing both `Content-Length` and `Transfer-Encoding` headers. (#2550)  - Fix invalid integer path parameters to return `400`. (#2551) (#2558)  - Fix `HttpResponseImpl` member updates not covering all fields. (#2573)  - Fix `const std::string_view` SQL binding. (#2571)  - Fix zero-length body handling when `Expect: 100-continue` is present. (#2560)  - Fix HTTP/1.0 keep-alive and sticky `closeConnection` handling for reused responses. (#2567)  - Fix `QueryBuilder` JOIN state when using single-table joins. (#2578)  - Fix SQLite connection reuse after failed transaction commit. (#2597)  - Fix `PubSub` topic name hashing randomness. (#2607)  - Fix `NUL` path rejection in static file routing. (#2600)  - Fix `RealIpResolver` IPv6 support. (#2598)  - Fix Redis event loop spinning on unhandled `POLLERR`. (#2608)  - Fix `Task` promise_type issue that caused Clang 21 SIGSEGV. (#2580)  - Fix `HttpClient` callback ownership after request timeout. (#2609)  - Fix `bad_weak_ptr` when initializing `DbClient` connections. (#2614)  - Fix MySQL teardown while a connection is still connecting. (#2615)  - Fix typed PostgreSQL query errors. (#2616)  - Fix PubSub callbacks to modify subscriptions safely. (#2620)  - Bound the PostgreSQL prepared statement cache to prevent unbounded growth. (#2618) 
```

### 2026-10-13T20:53:47Z [**huginn/huginn**](https://github.com/huginn/huginn)

```
# v2026.10.14  ## Changes  - Show maps of location Events with OpenStreetMap and Leaflet when UserLocationAgent has no Google Maps `api_key`, instead of showing no map.  [#3791](https://github.com/huginn/huginn/pull/3791) - Upgrade the PostgreSQL Docker Compose example from PostgreSQL 9.5 to 18.  [#3789](https://github.com/huginn/huginn/pull/3789) - Move the Heroku setup to the `heroku-24` stack and Heroku's native multi-buildpack support.  [#3788](https://github.com/huginn/huginn/pull/3788) - Remove the OpenShift and Cloud Foundry deployment files, whose platforms are no longer available.  [#3788](https://github.com/huginn/huginn/pull/3788) - Rewrite the README and deployment documentation around Docker, and add a [security policy](https://github.com/huginn/huginn/blob/v2026.10.14/SECURITY.md), a [contributing guide](https://github.com/huginn/huginn/blob/v2026.10.14/CONTRIBUTING.md), and a [sponsors page](https://github.com/huginn/huginn/blob/v2026.10.14/doc/sponsors.md).  [#3788](https://github.com/huginn/huginn/pull/3788) - Stop the bundled MySQL server in the multi-process image together with its child processes, so that leftover socket locks no longer prevent the next start. - Run the Docker specs against a release's images before tagging them with the release version.  ## Upgrading  If you run Huginn on Heroku, move the app to the `heroku-24` stack and replace `BUILDPACK_URL` with native buildpacks before November 2026, when Heroku stops building on `heroku-22`.  Updating Huginn does not change these settings.  See the [Heroku upgrade notes](https://github.com/huginn/huginn/blob/v2026.10.14/UPGRADING.md#moving-heroku-deployments-to-heroku-24).  If you started PostgreSQL from `docker/single-process/postgresql.yml`, keep your copy at `postgres:9.5` until you dump and restore the data into PostgreSQL 18.  A `postgres:18` container does not start on a 9.5 data directory.  See the [PostgreSQL upgrade notes](https://github.com/huginn/huginn/blob/v2026.10.14/UPGRADING.md#moving-the-postgresql-compose-example-to-postgresql-18).  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/huginn/huginn/compare/v2026.10.04...v2026.10.14 
```

# 5.8.48(2026-09-26) ### 🐣新特性 * 【core   】      `NumberUtil.toInt`增加长度校验（pr#4289@Github） * 【core   】      `FileUtil.subPath`增加重载，可选是否追踪链接（issue#IK3K5Z@Gitee） * 【core   】      添加`Base85`编码（pr#1474@Gitee）  ### 🐞Bug修复 * 【ai     】      Set hasProxy in BaseConfig.setProxy so a directly-set proxy is not ignored（pr#4291@Github） * 【ai     】      Serialize the streaming AI request body as UTF-8（pr#4294@Github） * 【ai     】      Attach generationConfig to the Gemini chatJson request（pr#4295@Github） * 【ai     】      Separate video parameter flag and value with a space（pr#4296@Github） * 【core   】      修复`NumberUtil`的range方法对step为0时未检查问题（pr#1463@Gitee） * 【socket 】      修复`ChannelUtil`的前缀，修复为`Hutool-socket-`（pr#1457@Gitee） * 【ai     】      Merge additional config into the Doubao tokenization request（pr#4300@Github） * 【ai     】      Ignore query string and fragment when detecting a media URL MIME type（pr#4299@Github） * 【ai     】      Build the Gemini upload URL with the upload path, not the full base URL（pr#4298@Github） * 【json   】      修复`JSONArray.toJSONObject`数组越界问题（pr#1455@Gitee） * 【core   】      修复`ClassUtil.getShortClassName`空指针问题（pr#1453@Gitee） * 【core   】      修复`Ftp.download`文件下载失败0字节文件残留问题（pr#4305@Github） * 【core   】      修复`AnnotationUtil`缓存key引起的缓存失效问题（issue#4310@Github） * 【core   】      修复`CsvParser.readLine()`跳过注释字符时没有更新`preChar`问题（pr#4321@Github） * 【core   】      修复`CsvParser`引号字段中连续 `\r` 的行号统计问题（pr#4324@Github） * 【core   】      修复`ReentrantCache`双重检查返回空值问题（pr#4325@Github） * 【core   】      修复`CharSequenceUtil.replaceFirst`在含增补字符时替换位置错误的问题（pr#4337@Github） * 【core   】      修复`Caesar.encode`负偏移量抛出异常及非字母表字符被静默损坏问题（pr#4327@Github） * 【core   】      修复`Convert.toInstant`转换目标类型写错、以及Map转LocalDateTime纳秒取错字段的问题（pr#4333@Github） * 【core   】      修复CRC16校验值随数据喂入方式变化的问题（pr#4335@Github）
